“All right, laugh,” said Sam.
“Sure, I’ll laugh,” said the Big Boy; “if you’d seen Steve’s face when he found out what it was all about, you’d split your pants laughing.”
“Steve’s the goods,” said Rico.
“You said a mouthful,” said the Big Boy, “he’s got them eating out of his hand. Well, I’m gonna beat it. You guys lay low and it might blow over. If things get hot, I’ll tip off Scabby and then you all better hit the rods. So long.”
===Chinese===
「嗐，你们这帮家伙是走运，自己还不知道。伍德那儿的经理吓得魂飞魄散，居然把一个便衣指认成了干内应的家伙。老天，真够呛！斯派克·里格（Spike Rieger）肺都气炸了。很快他就把经理钉死追问，那该死的蠢货说干这活儿的是几个波兰人。于是他们出去把史蒂夫·戈兰茨（Steve Gollancz）抓了起来。史蒂夫和他那伙人刚抢了一家银行，史蒂夫还当人家掌握了他的罪证。笑死人！」
大人物仰头放声大笑。萨姆·维托里烦躁地敲着桌子。
「行，你笑吧。」萨姆说。
「当然要笑，」大人物说；「你要是瞧见史蒂夫弄清是怎么回事时的那副脸，保管笑破裤子。」
「史蒂夫是块好料。」里科说。
「你说到点子上了，」大人物说，「他把那帮人治得服服帖帖。行了，我要走了。你们蛰伏着，兴许风头就过去了。事情吃紧，我会给斯卡比递话，那时候你们最好全都跑路。回见。」

===Original===
The Big Boy went out slamming the door. They heard him go downstairs; he walked as heavily as a squad of police and banged each step with his heels.
Rico went on with his game of solitaire.
“Well,” said Vettori, “something just tells me we’re gonna get ours.”
“Oh, hell!” said Rico, pushing the cards away from him, “I’d like to get the guy that invented that game.”
Vettori swore softly to himself at Rico’s indifference, then, pouring himself another drink, he said:
“You think Joe’s safe, Rico.”
===Chinese===
大人物出去，砰地摔上门。他们听见他下楼；他走得像一队警察那么沉，鞋跟把每一级台阶踩得山响。
里科接着玩他的单人牌。
「唉，」维托里说，「我心里有个声音说，咱们躲不过这一劫。」
「唉，见鬼！」里科说着，把牌推开，「我真想揪出发明这牌的那家伙。」
维托里为里科的漠然暗自轻声咒骂，然后又给自己倒了一盅，说：
「你觉得乔靠得住吗，里科。」

===Original===
“Yeah,” said Rico, “as long as they don’t nab him and put it to him. He can’t stand the gaff.”
“How about The Greek?”
Rico laughed.
“Safe as hell. Only thing with Otero, he gets lit and wants to raise hell. I had to knock him down a couple of times last night. He gets a little money and he goes nuts. That goddamn greaser never saw over five dollars all at once till I picked him up in Toledo. But he’s safe.”
“How about Tony?”
Rico didn’t say anything for a minute, but picked up his cards and began to shuffle them.
===Chinese===
「嗯，」里科说，「只要他们不抓住他逼供。他受不住那种折磨。」
「希腊人呢？」
里科笑了。
「稳当得很。奥特罗就一样，喝高了就要闹事。昨晚我不得不撂倒他两回。他兜里一有钱就发疯。那该死的南欧佬，我在托莱多（Toledo）把他捡回来之前，他一次见过的钱从没超过五块。不过他靠得住。」
「托尼呢？」
里科有一分钟没说话，只是拿起牌，开始洗。

===Original===
“I don’t know about Tony.”
Sam Vettori got up and walked back and forth, mopping his forehead at intervals with his big white silk handkerchief.
“Love of God, Rico, we can’t take no chances with him.”
Rico dealt out a couple of poker hands and began to play an imaginary game.
“You leave that to me, Sam,” he said.
Vettori put his hand on Rico’s shoulder.
===Chinese===
「托尼，我说不准。」
萨姆·维托里站起来，来回踱步，不时用那块白绸大手帕擦额头。
「看在老天爷份上，里科，他那儿可不能冒险。」
里科发了两副梭哈的牌，开始自己跟自己打。
「这事交给我，萨姆。」他说。
维托里把手搭在里科肩上。

===Original===
“That’s the talk, Rico. We get a break, we may come clean.” Vettori dropped back into his chair and poured himself another drink, but Rico reached across the table and pushed the glass off onto the floor.
“Slow down on that stuff, Sam. You got to keep your head clear.”
Vettori looked at Rico in a fury, then he lowered his eyes.
“You got the right dope, Rico. That stuff don’t do nobody no good.”
Vettori took the whisky bottle and locked it up in a cupboard.
===Chinese===
「这就对了，里科。捱过这一关，咱们就清了。」维托里跌回椅子里，又给自己倒了一盅，里科却隔着桌子伸手，把杯子推到了地上。
「那玩意儿少灌点，萨姆。你得让脑袋清醒。」
维托里狂怒地瞪着里科，然后垂下了眼睛。
「你说得在理，里科。那玩意儿对谁都没好处。」
维托里拿起威士忌酒瓶，锁进了柜子。

### II / 二

===Original===
About nine o’clock Carillo put his head in the door. Downstairs the jazz band had just started to play.
“Well?” demanded Vettori, getting to his feet.
“Blackie wants to see you,” said Carillo.
“All right.”
Carillo went out.
“What you suppose he wants?” said Vettori.
===Chinese===
九点来钟，卡里洛把头探进门来。楼下爵士乐队刚刚开演。
「怎么样？」维托里问，站起身来。
「布莱基（Blackie）要见你。」卡里洛说。
「行。」
卡里洛出去了。
「你猜他干什么来了？」维托里说。

===Original===
Rico, who was sitting with his chair tipped back against the wall reading a magazine, shook his head without looking up or answering. He was deep in the reading of a story about a rich society girl who fell in love with a bootlegger. Rico read everything he could find that had anything to do with society. He was fascinated by a stratum of existence which seemed so remote and unreal to him.
Blackie Avezzano, who managed Sam’s garage, came in and shut the door behind him. He was small and bowlegged, and he was so dark that he had been taken for a mulatto many times.
Vettori impatiently exclaimed:
“Well, what’s on your mind, Blackie?”
Rico went on reading his magazine. Blackie sat down at the table and seemed to be making an effort to collect his thoughts.
“All right, spit it out,” said Vettori.
===Chinese===
里科椅子往后翘着靠墙坐着看杂志，头也不抬，也不答话，只是摇了摇头。他正埋头读一篇故事，讲一个豪门的富家小姐爱上了一个私酒贩子。凡是跟上层社会沾边的文字，里科逮着什么读什么。那个阶层的生活对他来说遥远又虚幻，让他着迷。
布莱基·阿韦扎诺（Blackie Avezzano）——替萨姆照管车行的那位——进来，随手关上门。他个子矮小，罗圈腿，皮肤黑得多次被人当成黑白混血儿。
维托里不耐烦地喝道：
「行了，你有什么事，布莱基？」
里科接着看他的杂志。布莱基在桌边坐下，像是正努力把话理顺。
「行了，直说吧。」维托里说。

===Original===
Blackie couldn’t speak very good English, but as Rico didn’t know a word of Italian and Vettori preferred to speak English, he did the best he could.
“Tony, he took sick. Listen, I tell you, Tony he no know what. He took sick. I see him, listen, I tell you, what-you-say, he no got his guts. The Madre she send me call the doctore. Listen, he say, Tony, what-you-say, you been a drink. Now listen, you cut out a drink. That’s all. Tony he no drink. What a hell! One bottle of beer he can no drink. He no got his guts, that’s all.”
Vettori looked at Rico, who went on reading.
“Rico,” he said.
“I heard him,” said Rico, “I ain’t deaf.”
Blackie got up and stood twisting his cap in his hand. Vettori took out his billfold and handed Blackie a ten.
===Chinese===
布莱基英语说不好，可里科一个意大利字都不识，维托里又偏说英语，他只能尽力而为。
「托尼，他病了。听着，我告诉你，托尼他不知道怎么的。他病了。我看见他，听着，我告诉你，那个怎么说，他没胆了。妈妈（Madre）她叫我去请大夫（doctore）。听着，大夫说，托尼，那个怎么说，你喝多了。现在听着，你戒酒。就这样。托尼他不喝酒。搞什么名堂！一瓶啤酒他都喝不下。他没胆了，就这样。」
维托里看向里科，里科接着看杂志。
「里科。」他说。
「我听见了，」里科说，「我又不聋。」
布莱基站起来，手里绞着帽子。维托里掏出钱夹，递给布莱基一张十块的。

===Original===
“Blackie,” he said, “keep your eyes open, understand?”
“All right,” said Blackie, “I watch, see, I know. Tony no good. All right, I watch.”
When he had gone Rico said:
“Well, that’s that.”
“We can’t take no chances,” said Vettori.
“I’ll give him till tomorrow,” said Rico; “he can’t go far wrong with Blackie watching him. After that if he don’t settle down there won’t be no more Tony.”
===Chinese===
「布莱基，」他说，「把眼睛放亮，明白？」
「行，」布莱基说，「我盯着，瞧，我懂。托尼不顶事。行，我盯着。」
他走后里科说：
「行，就这么回事。」
「咱们不能冒险。」维托里说。
「我限他到明天，」里科说；「有布莱基盯着，他翻不了大浪。过了明天他要是还稳不下来，这世上就没有托尼了。」

### III / 三

===Original===
Tony had always been of a rather easygoing nature and took things as they came. His emotions, it is true, were very unstable; with him anger was almost immediately followed by a grin, and depression lasted only long enough for him to recognize that he had felt such an emotion. No, he had never before experienced the loneliness which is the result of continued despair. Now he felt it and it was too much for him. He looked back on the past as a sort of fabulous period when he had had peace of mind.
He could enjoy nothing. The fear of arrest and hanging dogged him even at the movies, formerly his chief pleasure; and in the company of Midge, his woman, he was so preoccupied that she thought he had a new woman and treated him accordingly.
Then he began to have attacks of acute indigestion, and it got so bad that the very sight of food was repugnant to him. He lost weight rapidly.
There was nothing he could do. He could not find one avenue of escape. But little by little the thought of Father McConagha took possession of him. Tony was too unintelligent to know that what he needed most of all was someone he could unburden himself to. But he blundered toward that solution.
Blackie’s solicitude helped some. Blackie came to see him every night; and once, when Tony’s indigestion had been worse than usual, he had even gone for the doctor.
Tony’s mother put her hand on his shoulder.
===Chinese===
托尼天性一向随和，遇事听其自然。不错，他的情绪很不稳定；在他身上，怒气几乎立刻就会化作一笑，消沉也只持续到他察觉自己有过这么一阵情绪为止。不，他从前从没尝过这种持续的绝望酿成的孤独。现在他尝到了，这让他受不了。回望过去，那简直是一段神话般的岁月，那时他还有过内心的安宁。
他什么也享受不了。对被捕和上绞架的恐惧连看电影时都缠着他——看电影原是他最大的乐趣；跟他的女人米琪在一起，他也是满腹心事，弄得她以为他有了新欢，便拿那副脸色对他。
接着他开始一阵阵剧烈地消化不良，严重到一看见饭菜就恶心。他的体重飞快往下掉。
他毫无办法。他找不到一条脱身的路。但麦科纳加神父的影子一点一点攫住了他。托尼脑子不够使，不明白自己最需要的是一个能倾诉的人。可他跌跌撞撞地撞向了这个出路。
布莱基的照应多少有点用处。布莱基每晚来看他；有一回托尼的消化不良比平时更重，他甚至跑去请了大夫。
托尼的母亲把手搭在他肩上。

===Original===
“Antonio,” she said, “I think I’ll go across the street and see Mrs. Mangia. She is having a new baby. Only think! That will be twelve.”
Tony tried to smile.
“Twelve!” said Tony’s mother, shaking her head slowly from side to side, “and one is too much.”
“A bad egg like me is.”
“You ain’t a bad egg, Antonio,” said his mother, “you are only lazy.”
Tony said nothing.
===Chinese===
「安东尼奥，」她说，「我想去街对面看看曼贾太太（Mrs. Mangia）。她又添了个娃。想想看！这就十二个了。」
托尼想笑一笑。
「十二个！」托尼的母亲说，缓缓地左右摇头，「一个都嫌多。」
「像我这样的坏种就嫌多。」
「你不是坏种，安东尼奥，」他母亲说，「你就是懒。」
托尼没说话。

===Original===
“Listen, Antonio, I left some spaghetti on the stove. If you feel better eat some. You don’t want to get all run down.”
“All right,” said Tony.
Tony’s mother went out. As soon as the door was shut, Tony wished that she hadn’t gone. He was afraid. At the sound of footsteps in the corridor, he felt his hair rise and beads of sweat stood out on his forehead.
Blackie put his head in the door.
“How do you feel, Tony?” he asked.
“Hello, Blackie,” said Tony, “come on in and have a smoke.” Blackie took a cigarette from the proffered pack and sat down. While he smoked he kept glancing at Tony.
===Chinese===
「听着，安东尼奥，我在炉子上留了点意大利面。你要是好受些就吃点。可别把自己拖垮了。」
「行。」托尼说。
托尼的母亲出去了。门一关上，托尼就后悔让她走了。他害怕。走廊里一有脚步声，他就觉得头发根根倒竖，额头上沁出汗珠。
布莱基把头探进门来。
「你觉得怎么样，托尼？」他问。
「你好，布莱基，」托尼说，「进来抽口烟。」布莱基从递过来的烟盒里抽了一支，坐下。抽烟的工夫他不停地瞟托尼。

===Original===
“Whatsa mat, Tony?” said Blackie. “You ain’t look so good.” Tony stared at Blackie for a moment, then he began to shake all over.
“Jesus, Blackie,” he cried, “I can’t stand it. They’ll get us sure. Have you seen tonight’s paper?”
Blackie shrugged.
“I can no read.”
“It’s all up with us,” said Tony. “My God, I don’t see how Rico stands it.”
“Rico no scared.”
===Chinese===
「怎么了，托尼？」布莱基说。「你气色不好。」托尼盯着布莱基看了片刻，然后浑身抖了起来。
「老天，布莱基，」他喊道，「我撑不住了。他们准会抓到我们。你看今晚的报纸了吗？」
布莱基耸耸肩。
「我不认字。」
「咱们全完了，」托尼说。「老天，我不明白里科怎么受得住。」
「里科不怕。」

===Original===
“Well, he ought to be. He’s the one that done it.”
Blackie shrugged.
“No can help. What-you-say, Cortenni pull a gat. No can help.”
Tony suddenly became very pale. He heard an automobile stop in the street below. He ran to the window and looked down, then he turned and came back.
“I thought it was the cops,” he said.
“Look,” said Blackie, “you no better be sick. Listen, you no got your guts, Tony. Rico say, be a man. That is good. Be a man, Rico say. You no better be sick.”
===Chinese===
「哼，他该怕。开枪的是他。」
布莱基耸耸肩。
「没法子。那个怎么说，考特尼拔枪了。没法子。」
托尼突然脸色煞白。他听见楼下的街上有汽车停下。他跑到窗口往下看，然后转身回来。
「我还当是警察。」他说。
「我说，」布莱基说，「你最好别病。听着，你没胆了，托尼。里科说，做个男子汉。那就对。做个男子汉，里科说。你最好别病。」

===Original===
“The hell with Rico,” said Tony.
Blackie shrugged.
Tony stood in the middle of the room for a minute or two looking at the floor, then, suddenly making up his mind, he went over to the hatrack and got his hat.
“Where you go?” asked Blackie.
Tony hesitated.
“I go too,” said Blackie.
===Chinese===
「去他妈的里科。」托尼说。
布莱基耸耸肩。
托尼在屋子中间站了一两分钟，眼睛看着地板，然后猛地拿定主意，走到衣帽架前取下帽子。
「你去哪儿？」布莱基问。
托尼犹豫了。
「我也去。」布莱基说。

===Original===
“No, you go home,” said Tony, then looking steadily at Blackie he said: “Me, I’m going down to St. Dominick’s and see Father McConagha.”
“What,” cried Blackie, leaping up in alarm. “Tony, my God, you no tell him nothing.”
“I got to,” cried Tony vehemently.
Blackie took hold of Tony’s arm.
“Tony, my boy, don’t go. Listen. Tony, you no sick. Be a man. Hear what I tell you. You no live, see, you no live. Be a man.”
Tony pushed him away.
===Chinese===
「不，你回家去，」托尼说，然后定定地看着布莱基说：「我嘛，我要去圣多米尼克教堂，见麦科纳加神父。」
「什么，」布莱基喊道，惊慌地跳起来。「托尼，老天，你什么也别跟他说。」
「我非说不可。」托尼激动地喊道。
布莱基抓住托尼的胳膊。
「托尼，我的小子，别去。听着。托尼，你没病。做个男子汉。听见我的话。你活不成，瞧，你活不成。做个男子汉。」
托尼推开他。

===Original===
“You go home, Blackie.”
Tony went out. Blackie heard him walking slowly down the corridor. When he could no longer hear his footsteps, he leapt to his feet, opened a back window, went down the fire-escape, and took a shortcut through the alleys. He knocked at the back door of the Palermo and Carillo let him in.
===Chinese===
「你回家去，布莱基。」
托尼出去了。布莱基听见他慢慢走过走廊。等再听不见脚步声，他一跃而起，推开一扇后窗，下了防火梯，抄小巷的近路。他敲响帕勒莫的后门，卡里洛放他进去。

### IV / 四

===Original===
Vettori stared at Rico, who said nothing.
“Crazy! Crazy!” said Blackie. “I tell him, be a man, be a man. But he say, I got to, I got to.”
Rico hastily put on his overcoat.
“Well, I guess that’s it,” said Sam Vettori.
“Yeah,” said Rico, “that’s it. Now get yourself a can, Sam, and let’s go. We ain’t got any time to waste.”
Vettori rubbed both hands over his face.
===Chinese===
维托里盯着里科，里科一言不发。
「疯了！疯了！」布莱基说。「我告诉他，做男子汉，做男子汉。可他说，我非去不可，我非去不可。」
里科匆忙穿上大衣。
「唉，我看就这么定了。」萨姆·维托里说。
「是，」里科说，「就这么定了。去弄辆车，萨姆，走。咱们没工夫耽搁。」
维托里双手抹了把脸。

===Original===
“Not me,” he said.
Rico looked at him.
“Take Blackie,” said Vettori.
Blackie implored them with his eyes.
“Blackie’s no good,” said Rico.
“No,” said Blackie, “I no good.”
===Chinese===
「我不去。」他说。
里科看着他。
「带布莱基去。」维托里说。
布莱基用眼睛哀求他们。
「布莱基不顶用。」里科说。
「对，」布莱基说，「我不顶用。」

===Original===
Carillo put his head in the door.
“Reilley’s downstairs, boss.”
“Take Carillo,” said Vettori.
Carillo stared at them suspiciously. Rico leapt across the room and grabbed him by the arm.
“Listen, Bat, can you drive a can?”
“Sure.”
===Chinese===
卡里洛把头探进门来。
「雷利在楼下，老板。」
「带卡里洛去。」维托里说。
卡里洛狐疑地盯着他们。里科一步跃过屋子，抓住他的胳膊。
「听着，巴特，你会开车吗？」
「会。」

===Original===
“Will you let her out when I office you?”
“Sure.”
“All right, let’s go.”
“Take that black roadster, Carillo,” said Vettori, “but for God’s sake don’t smash it up.”
Carillo ran out leaving the door open. Rico walked over and closed the door, then he said:
“Sam, you ain’t got any more guts than Tony. Now listen, get down there and talk turkey to Reilley. Get that! By God, I guess I got to boss this job myself.”
===Chinese===
「我一发话，你就放开了跑，行吗？」
「行。」
「好，走。」
「开那辆黑色双座跑车，卡里洛，」维托里说，「可看在老天爷份上，别撞坏了。」
卡里洛跑出去，门也没关。里科走过去关上门，然后说：
「萨姆，你的胆子不比托尼多多少。现在听着，下去跟雷利把话挑明。记住！老天作证，看来这活儿得我亲自当家。」

===Original===
Vettori looked at Rico with hatred. But he said:
“All right, Rico, you’re the boss now.”
Rico went out. Blackie said:
“Goodbye Tony!”
Carillo was waiting with the black roadster in the alleyway. Rico jumped in and the roadster leapt away. Carillo took a turn on two wheels.
“It’s a cinch he went the shortest cut,” said Rico.
===Chinese===
维托里带着恨意看里科。可他说：
「行，里科，现在你当家。」
里科出去了。布莱基说：
「别了，托尼！」
卡里洛开着黑色双座跑车在巷子里等着。里科跳上车，跑车蹿了出去。卡里洛两轮着地拐过一个弯。
「他准是抄的近路。」里科说。

===Original===
“Sure,” said Carillo, “I know what I’m doing.”
“All right,” said Rico, “do it.”
The wind had risen and it began to snow, big, heavy flakes which sailed past the street lights. In a few minutes the ground was covered.
Carillo took the shortest cut and Rico, holding his big automatic on the seat beside him, sat straining his eyes. But there was no sign of Tony.
“If we miss him, I’ll kick hell out of Blackie,” said Rico.
“Keep your shirt on, boss,” said Carillo.
===Chinese===
「那当然，」卡里洛说，「我知道自己在干什么。」
「行，」里科说，「抄。」
风大了起来，下起了雪，大片大片的雪片从路灯前飘过。不消几分钟，地上就白了。
卡里洛抄了近路，里科把大号自动手枪搁在旁边座位上，坐直了眯眼张望。可不见托尼的影子。
「要是让他溜了，我把布莱基踢个半死。」里科说。
「沉住气，老板。」卡里洛说。

===Original===
The tall spires of St. Dominick’s rose before them at the end of the block. The street was deserted. Carillo drove slowly now, hugging the curb. In a moment he pointed:
“There’s a guy.”
Rico leaned forward.
“Take it easy, Bat,” he said, “I think it’s Tony.”
“Tony,” called Rico.
“Yeah?” came Tony’s voice. “Who is it?”
===Chinese===
街区尽头，圣多米尼克教堂的高高尖塔耸立在他们面前。街上空无一人。卡里洛放慢车速，贴着路边石开。过了一会儿他指了指：
「有个人。」
里科俯身向前。
「别急，巴特，」他说，「我看是托尼。」
「托尼。」里科喊了一声。
「嗯？」传来托尼的声音。「谁呀？」

===Original===
Rico fired. A long spur of flame shot out in the darkness. Rico emptied his gun. Tony fell without a sound.
“All right now, Bat,” said Rico, “let her out.”
===Chinese===
里科开枪了。一道长长的火舌在黑暗中喷出。里科打空了整梭子弹。托尼一声不吭地倒了下去。
「行了，巴特，」里科说，「放开了跑。」

### V / 五

===Original===
Joe and Olga were sitting in a quiet corner of a Gold Coast hotel dining-room. They were waiting for their dessert. Joe, comfortably full and inclined to be amiable, sat looking at Olga. She was the goods. Of course he stepped out with other broads occasionally when Olga was busy, but that didn’t count. Olga was the goods and she was his woman. Other men didn’t rate with her, that’s all. He studied her. There she sat with her round dark face, her high cheekbones, and her dark mascaraed eyes.
“Well,” said Olga, “take a good look.”
“Listen, baby,” said Joe, “you got it. I ain’t kidding. You got everything. There ain’t a woman in Chicago that’s got half your stuff. You make ’em all look silly.”
Olga reached across the table and patted his hand.
“I don’t believe it, but say it again. I like it.”
“No fooling.”
===Chinese===
乔和奥尔加坐在黄金海岸（Gold Coast）一家酒店餐厅的僻静角落里，等着上甜点。乔吃得舒舒服服，心平气和，坐在那儿端详奥尔加。她是块好料。当然，奥尔加忙的时候，他偶尔也带别的娘们儿出去逛，可那不算数。奥尔加是块好料，她是他的女人。别的男人跟她比不了，就这么回事。他端详着她。她就坐在那儿，圆圆的黑脸盘，高高的颧骨，涂着黑睫毛膏的眼睛。
「怎么，」奥尔加说，「看个够吧。」
「听着，宝贝，」乔说，「你有的是。我不瞎说。你什么都有。全芝加哥的女人，本事抵得上你一半的都没有。你让她们全都成了笑话。」
奥尔加隔着桌子伸手拍了拍他的手。
「我不信，不过再说一遍。我爱听。」
「不骗你。」

===Original===
“What a line,” said Olga.
The waiter brought their dessert.
“I’ll tell you,” said Olga, looking at her wrist watch, “let’s go to a movie. I got time.”
Joe didn’t like movies very well; all that soppy love stuff! But now he wanted to please Olga.
“All right. Where’ll it be?”
Olga turned to the waiter.
===Chinese===
「多会说话。」奥尔加说。
侍者端来了甜点。
「这么着吧，」奥尔加说着，看了看腕表，「咱们去看场电影。我有工夫。」
乔不大喜欢看电影；全是那套黏黏糊糊的爱情玩意儿！可这会儿他想哄奥尔加高兴。
「行。去哪家？」
奥尔加转向侍者。

===Original===
“Bring us a paper, please.”
The waiter brought a paper and handed it to Joe. He unfolded it and started to turn to the theatrical page, but instead he read with absorption an article on the front page. Olga saw him swallow several times. When he glanced up at her there was a bewildered look in his eyes and his face begun to get pale.
“What’s wrong?” she asked.
“They got Tony,” said Joe.
“Who?”
“I don’t know. Rico, I guess. He must have turned yellow.”
===Chinese===
「请给我们拿份报来。」
侍者拿来一份报，递给乔。他展开报纸，本想翻到戏剧版，却一下被头版的一篇文章吸住了。奥尔加看见他咽了好几次口水。他抬眼看她时，眼里一片茫然，脸色开始发白。
「怎么了？」她问。
「他们把托尼干掉了。」乔说。
「谁干的？」
「不知道。我猜是里科。托尼准是怂了。」

===Original===
Joe ran his hand across his forehead, then he took out his gold cigarette case, but without ostentation this time and lit a cigarette. Olga took the paper from him. She read:

> Antonio Passalacqua, known as Tony Passa, reputed to be a member of the Vettori gang, was found dead near the steps of St. Dominick’s Cathedral﻿ ﻿… as far as the police can ascertain no one saw him killed﻿ ﻿… when questioned Sam Vettori denied all knowledge of the shooting and intimated that it was the work of a rival gang﻿ ﻿… police say that this is likely.
===Chinese===
乔用手抹过额头，然后掏出金烟盒——这回没摆弄给人看——点上一支烟。奥尔加从他手里拿过报纸。她读到：

> 安东尼奥·帕萨尔夸（Antonio Passalacqua），人称托尼·帕萨，据传系维托里帮成员，被发现死于圣多米尼克大教堂台阶附近……据警方查明，无人目击其被杀……萨姆·维托里接受询问时对枪击一事矢口否认，暗示系敌对帮派所为……警方称此说可信。

===Original===
“Jesus!” said Joe.
Olga turned quickly to the theatrical page.
“Joe, honey,” she said, “there’s a good comedy at the Oriental. What do you say?”
Joe crumpled up his cigarette and put it in the ashtray.
“Boy, Rico didn’t waste no time with him.”
“Joe, don’t you want to see that comedy?”
===Chinese===
「老天！」乔说。
奥尔加赶紧翻到戏剧版。
「乔，亲爱的，」她说，「东方剧院（Oriental）有部好的喜剧片。去不去？」
乔把烟捏灭，放进烟灰缸。
「好家伙，里科对他可真没耽搁工夫。」
「乔，你不想看那部喜剧吗？」

===Original===
“Sure,” said Joe, “let’s go see it.”
Joe sat silent in the taxi all the way to the theatre. As they were getting out, he said:
“Boy, that Rico is sure careless with a rod.”
“Forget it, honey,” said Olga.
===Chinese===
「想，」乔说，「走，去看。」
去剧院的一路，乔坐在出租车里没说话。下车时他说：
「好家伙，那个里科使枪可真不当回事。」
「别想了，亲爱的。」奥尔加说。

### VI / 六

===Original===
When Rico came in, Seal Skin was sitting in a chair by the window and Otero was lying on the bed without his shirt, singing loudly. Rico walked over and put his hand on Seal Skin’s shoulder.
“Listen,” he said, “I thought you told me you was gonna look after The Greek?”
“I can’t do nothing with him,” said Seal Skin.
Rico went over to the bed and looked at Otero.
“Señor Rico,” cried Otero, “listen, I will sing for you.” Rico turned.
“Seal,” he said, “that bird’s gonna spill something if you don’t keep him sober.”
===Chinese===
里科进来时，海豹皮正坐在窗边的椅子上，奥特罗光着膀子躺在床上，大声唱着歌。里科走过去，把手搭在海豹皮肩上。
「听着，」他说，「我记得你说过会看住希腊人的？」
「我拿他没法子。」海豹皮说。
里科走到床边，看着奥特罗。
「里科先生（Señor Rico），」奥特罗喊道，「听着，我给你唱歌。」里科转过身。
「海豹，」他说，「你不让他保持清醒，那家伙早晚要漏嘴。」

===Original===
“Listen,” said Seal Skin. “I ain’t no nurse. A guy ought to look out for himself. What the hell can I do, anyway? I can’t knock him cold.”
“You never did have much sense,” said Rico.
“All right, wise boy. Let’s see what you can do.”
Rico took off his overcoat.
“Got any ice?”
“Sure,” said Seal Skin without moving.
===Chinese===
「听着，」海豹皮说。「我又不是看护。男人自己得管住自己。再说我到底能怎么样？总不能把他打昏吧。」
「你从来就没多少脑子。」里科说。
「行啊，机灵鬼。倒要看看你有什么办法。」
里科脱下大衣。
「有冰吗？」
「有。」海豹皮说，动也不动。

===Original===
“Well, goddamn it, get on your feet and get it.”
Seal Skin was afraid of Rico but she didn’t want him to suspect it. She got to her feet leisurely, picked up one of Otero’s big cigars, lit it, and stood puffing. Then, having demonstrated her lack of fear, she went to the kitchen for the ice.
Rico went over to the bed.
“Otero,” he said, “have you got any liquor around here?”
“What do I care for liquor!” cried Otero. “I will sing for you.” Rico slapped Otero’s face.
“A hell of a crew I’m mixed up with,” he said.
===Chinese===
「那就他妈站起来去拿。」
海豹皮怕里科，但不想让他看出来。她慢悠悠地站起来，拿起奥特罗的一支粗雪茄点上，站着喷了几口。就这样表明了自己不怕之后，她才去厨房取冰。
里科走到床边。
「奥特罗，」他说，「你这儿还有酒吗？」
「我要酒干什么！」奥特罗喊道。「我给你唱歌。」里科一巴掌抽在奥特罗脸上。
「我搅和进的这伙人可真够呛。」他说。

===Original===
Otero looked at him, startled.
“What is wrong with me?”
“You’re a dirty yellow bum.”
“I am not a yellow bum,” cried Otero, trying to sit up.
Rico struck him hard this time, knocking him back on the bed. Otero put his hand to his face and looked at Rico.
“If you got any more liquor here you better tell me where it is,” said Rico.
===Chinese===
奥特罗吃了一惊，看着他。
「我怎么啦？」
「你是个邋遢的怂包软蛋。」
「我不是怂包软蛋。」奥特罗喊着想坐起来。
这回里科下手重，一拳把他打回床上。奥特罗捂着脸，看着里科。
「你这儿要是还有酒，最好告诉我搁哪儿了。」里科说。

===Original===
Otero reached under his pillow and pulled out a quart bottle over half full. Rico slipped it into his pocket.
Otero’s face grew red.
“Rico,” he said, “you give me back my liquor.”
He tried to sit up, but Rico hit him and he fell back. Seal Skin came in with a couple of pieces of ice wrapped in a towel.
“What the hell you want to beat him up for?” she said.
“I’m gonna get him sober and keep him that way.”
===Chinese===
奥特罗伸手到枕头底下，掏出一瓶多半满的一夸脱装酒。里科把它塞进自己口袋。
奥特罗的脸涨红了。
「里科，」他说，「把酒还我。」
他想坐起来，里科一拳把他打回去。海豹皮拿着毛巾裹的几块冰进来。
「你他妈干吗揍他？」她说。
「我要让他醒酒，还得让他一直醒着。」

===Original===
“Yeah? Well, you’re gonna have a full-time job.”
Rico took the ice, a piece in each hand, and began to rub it over Otero’s face and chest. He rubbed hard and it hurt Otero, who struggled.
“Rico,” he said, “what have I done to you? Rico, you are my friend. Why do you treat me this way?”
“He’ll be bawling next,” said Seal Skin.
Suddenly, Otero became very angry and struggled so fiercely that he threw Rico off and climbed out of bed. The ice clattered to the floor. Rico took one step towards him and set himself for a punch, but Seal Skin grabbed his arm.
“For God’s sake let up on him,” she cried, “ain’t he in bad enough shape?”
===Chinese===
「是吗？那你这活儿可有得干了。」
里科接过冰，一手一块，开始往奥特罗的脸上和胸口上蹭。他蹭得很重，奥特罗疼得挣扎。
「里科，」他说，「我哪里对不住你？里科，你是我的朋友。你为什么这样待我？」
「下一步就该嚎了。」海豹皮说。
突然，奥特罗勃然大怒，挣得凶极了，一把甩开里科，翻下床来。冰块哗啦啦掉在地板上。里科朝他迈了一步，摆开架势要打，海豹皮却抓住了他的胳膊。
「看在老天爷份上，饶了他吧，」她喊道，「他那副样子还不够惨吗？」

===Original===
Rico was furious. He slapped Seal Skin across the face with his open hand.
“A fine bunch of yellow bellies and squealers I’m mixed up with,” he cried. “Listen, idiot, ain’t he a meal ticket? You want the black wagon to come and haul him away?”
Otero reeled across the room. Rico leapt after him and knocked him to the floor. Otero raised his head.
“Rico,” he said, “what have I done to you?”
Rico picked up the ice and kneeling down beside Otero began to rub him with it, harder than before. Otero gasped.
“Listen,” said Rico, “you got to get sober. I’m your friend, Otero, do you get what I’m saying? You got to sober up and stay that way.”
===Chinese===
里科狂怒。他抡开巴掌，抽在海豹皮脸上。
「我搅和进的这伙人，全是怂包和告密的货色。」他吼道。「听着，蠢货，他不是你的摇钱树吗？你想让黑囚车来把他拉走？」
奥特罗踉跄着往屋子那头走。里科追过去，把他打倒在地板上。奥特罗抬起头。
「里科，」他说，「我哪里对不住你？」
里科捡起冰块，跪在奥特罗身边，拿冰往他身上蹭，比刚才更重。奥特罗直喘。
「听着，」里科说，「你必须醒过来。我是你的朋友，奥特罗，明白我的话吗？你必须醒酒，还得一直醒着。」

===Original===
Tears ran down Otero’s cheeks.
“All right, Rico,” he said.
In half an hour, Rico had him sober. Seal Skin was sitting with her feet on the window sill, smoking one of Otero’s big cigars. Otero sat pale and shaken, looking at Rico.
“Well, big boy,” said Seal Skin, “I got to hand it to you. You done it.”
Rico smiled. Then he took out his billfold and handed Seal Skin a ten.
“There’s a little cush for you. You ain’t sore at me cause I socked you, are you? I got red hot mad, that’s all.”
===Chinese===
眼泪顺着奥特罗的脸颊往下淌。
「好吧，里科。」他说。
半小时后，里科让他清醒了。海豹皮两脚搁在窗台上，抽着奥特罗的一支粗雪茄。奥特罗苍白地坐着，浑身发颤，眼睛望着里科。
「怎么样，大人物，」海豹皮说，「我服了你。你办到了。」
里科笑了。然后他掏出钱夹，递给海豹皮一张十块的。
「给你一点甜头。不因为我揍了你记恨吧？我当时火冒三丈，就这么回事。」

===Original===
“You didn’t sock me hard,” said Seal Skin, “but it was ten dollars’ worth.”
Otero didn’t have much to say. He sat looking at the floor, ashamed of himself.
“How do you feel?” asked Rico.
“Me, not so good,” said Otero.
“Want a little drink?”
Otero looked at Rico, not trusting him, then he nodded. Rico handed him the bottle.
===Chinese===
「你揍得不算重，」海豹皮说，「可也值十块钱了。」
奥特罗没什么可说的。他坐在那儿看着地板，为自己害臊。
「觉得怎么样？」里科问。
「我，不怎么样。」奥特罗说。
「想来一口吗？」
奥特罗看着里科，不大信他，然后点点头。里科把酒瓶递给他。

===Original===
“I said little drink,” cautioned Rico.
Otero took a swallow and handed back the bottle.
“Now,” said Rico, “get your clothes on and we’ll take a look at Tony.”
===Chinese===
「我说的是一小口。」里科叮嘱道。
奥特罗咽下一口，把瓶子递回去。
「现在，」里科说，「穿上衣服，咱们去看看托尼。」

### VII / 七

===Original===
There were many rumours in Little Italy about the passing of Sam Vettori. The full truth, of course, was only guessed at, but the simple facts were known. Sam Vettori’s star was setting, Rico’s was rising. Rico had always been right; there was never any question of that. Rico had always inspired fear. But now, as the probable head of a big minor gang whose activities were varied and whose yearly income was enormous, his potentialities were prodigiously increased and he was treated accordingly.
When he entered Tony’s flat, several members of the Vettori gang, sitting near the door, got up and offered him their chairs. He merely shook his head and walked across to where Sam Vettori was sitting. Otero, who had entered a little behind Rico, stopped to talk with Blackie Avezzano.
Carillo brought a chair for Rico and Rico sat down beside Sam Vettori.
“We’re going to plant the kid right,” said Vettori, “that’ll look good.”
Rico stared across the room at a large horseshoe wreath which bore the single word: Tony. That was his contribution.
“Sure,” said Rico.
===Chinese===
关于萨姆·维托里的失势，小意大利里流言四起。全部真相当然只能靠猜，但简单的事实人人皆知。萨姆·维托里的星正在沉落，里科的正在升起。里科一向是对的；这一点从无异议。里科一向叫人害怕。而如今，他眼看要成为一个大二流帮派的头目，那帮派营生五花八门，年收入数目惊人，他的分量暴涨，旁人待他也随之变了。
他走进托尼的公寓时，坐在门边的几个维托里帮成员站起来，把座位让给他。他只是摇摇头，径直朝萨姆·维托里坐的地方走去。奥特罗跟在里科身后一点进来，停下跟布莱基·阿韦扎诺说话。
卡里洛给里科搬来一把椅子，里科在萨姆·维托里身边坐下。
「咱们要把这孩子风风光光地安葬，」维托里说，「那样好看。」
里科望着屋子那头一个巨大的马蹄莲花圈，上面只有两个字：托尼。那是他送的。
「当然。」里科说。

===Original===
He was a little uneasy. Not that he felt any remorse. What he had done was merely an act of policy. A man in this game must be a man. If he gets yellow, why, there’s only one remedy for it.
“They sure fix ’em up good now,” said Vettori, nodding in the direction of the coffin; “he don’t look dead. He looks like he was asleep.”
“Yeah?” said Rico.
“It beats me how they do it,” said Vettori. Carillo came across the room and whispered to Rico and Vettori.
“Two bulls in the hallway.”
“They coming in?” asked Rico.
===Chinese===
他有点不自在。倒不是出于悔恨。他干的事不过是一步棋。干这一行的男人就得是条汉子。要是怂了，那就只有一个疗法。
「现在他们把死人收拾得可真像样，」维托里说着，朝棺材的方向点了点头；「他不像死了。倒像睡着了。」
「是吗？」里科说。
「我真想不透他们是怎么弄的。」维托里说。卡里洛穿过屋子走过来，对里科和维托里低语。
「走廊里有两个条子。」
「要进来吗？」里科问。

===Original===
“No, just standing there.”
“All right.”
There was a movement at the door. Mrs. Passalacqua came in between two of her friends. She had been at St. Dominick’s for over an hour. Rico got up and offered her his chair. One of the women helped her off with her hat. She sat down. Her grey hair was parted in the middle and drawn tightly down; her face was a dead white.
Rico walked over to look at Tony. At the head of the coffin were two big candles, one of them leaning a little and dripping tallow. Tony lay with his hands folded. Rico looked down. Somehow he had expected Tony to be changed. He was not. Here lay the same Tony who used to play poker with such fury. The same Tony, yes, only dead.
Carillo put his hand on Rico’s shoulder.
“Bulls want to see you, boss.”
===Chinese===
「不，就站在那儿。」
「行。」
门口一阵动静。帕萨尔夸太太（Mrs. Passalacqua）在两个女友的搀扶下走进来。她在圣多米尼克教堂待了一个多钟头。里科起身把椅子让给她。一个女人帮她摘下帽子。她坐下了。灰白的头发中分，紧紧地向后梳拢；脸上一片死白。
里科走过去看托尼。棺材头部点着两支大蜡烛，其中一支有点歪，滴着烛泪。托尼双手交叠躺着。里科低头看。不知怎么，他原以为托尼会有所不同。并没有。躺在这儿的还是那个打起梭哈来那么凶的托尼。还是那个托尼，是的，只是死了。
卡里洛把手搭在里科肩上。
「条子要见你，老板。」

===Original===
Rico nodded.
“They want you to come out in the hall.”
“All right,” said Rico, turning away from the coffin, “tell Otero.”
Otero came over beside Rico and stood looking at Tony.
“Listen,” said Rico, “this may be a pinch. I don’t know. If it is, I’ll go with them. They ain’t got nothing on me. But if there’s any trouble, Scabby’ll keep you posted. Ma’s got my jack, see?”
“All right,” said Otero.
===Chinese===
里科点点头。
「他们要你出来，到走廊去。」
「行。」里科说着，从棺材边转过身，「告诉奥特罗。」
奥特罗走过来站在里科身边，望着托尼。
「听着，」里科说，「这可能是要抓人。说不准。真是抓，我就跟他们走。他们抓不到我什么把柄。可要是出了事，斯卡比会随时给你递信。我的钱在大妈手里，明白？」
「行。」奥特罗说。

===Original===
Rico started across the room and Otero followed him. Before Rico reached the door, Tony’s mother suddenly put her hands to her face and began to sob wildly.
“Oh, Tony, Tony!” she cried.
The women who had come in with her tried to quieten her, but she pushed them away, and, rising, walked over to the coffin and stood looking down at Tony. Then, still sobbing, she let the women lead her into the next room.
“That’s a woman for you,” said Rico.
“Well,” said Otero, shrugging. “Tony was her son.”
The hallway was lined with poor Italians who, not knowing the Passalacquas, had come out of curiosity. They stood in silent groups, trying to peep in through the open door. Women in disreputable housedresses carrying dirty children; pregnant women; old men with crinkly grey hair and seamed brown faces; young girls trying to look up-to-date and American. When Rico came out they all stared at him.
===Chinese===
里科往屋子那头走，奥特罗跟着。里科还没到门口，托尼的母亲突然双手捂脸，失声痛哭起来。
「噢，托尼，托尼！」她哭喊。
陪她来的女人们想劝住她，她却推开她们，站起身，走到棺材前，低头望着托尼。然后，仍然抽泣着，由女人们搀进了隔壁房间。
「女人就是这样。」里科说。
「唉，」奥特罗耸耸肩。「托尼是她儿子。」
走廊两边站满了贫穷的意大利人，他们并不认识帕萨尔夸家的人，纯粹是好奇来的。他们一群群默不作声地站着，想从敞开的门往里张望。穿寒酸家常裙、抱着脏孩子的女人；怀了孕的女人；一头卷曲灰发、棕色脸上满是皱纹的老头；想把自己扮得时髦点、美国化的年轻姑娘。里科一出来，他们都盯着他看。

===Original===
Flaherty took hold of his arm.
“Rico,” he said, “come down to the end of the hall. I want to see you a minute.”
“Is this a pinch?” asked Rico. Flaherty laughed.
“Got a bad conscience, have you? Well, you ought to have.” Rico noticed that the other detective, whom he had never seen before, kept staring at him. Rico planted his feet firmly and stared back.
“What’s the big idea, Flaherty?” he asked.
“Well,” said Flaherty, “just to put your mind at rest. I’ll tell you, this ain’t a pinch. It ought to be, but it ain’t. Now will you take a walk﻿ ﻿… ?”
===Chinese===
弗莱厄蒂抓住他的胳膊。
「里科，」他说，「到走廊那头去。我想跟你说句话。」
「这是要抓人？」里科问。弗莱厄蒂笑了。
「心里有鬼，是吧？嗯，也该有了。」里科注意到另一个警探一直在盯着他，那人他从没见过。里科双脚站稳，瞪了回去。
「到底什么意思，弗莱厄蒂？」他问。
「唉，」弗莱厄蒂说，「就是让你安安心。我告诉你，这不是抓人。本该是，可不是。怎么样，走几步……？」

===Original===
“Sure,” said Rico.
Otero came out into the hallway and stood watching them. Rico went down to the end of the hall with the two plainclothes men. Some of the poor Italians followed them and stood staring. But Flaherty motioned them off as if he were shooing chickens.
“Beat it,” he said; “go tend to your own business.”
They moved away slowly, looking back.
“All right,” said Rico, “let’s have it.”
Flaherty took out a big cigar and began chewing on it. The other man kept staring. Rico was puzzled and wondered what the game was; then he noticed that the light at their end of the hall was good, much better than any other place in the hall. The once-over? Well, what then?
===Chinese===
「行。」里科说。
奥特罗出来站到走廊里，看着他们。里科跟两个便衣走到走廊尽头。几个穷意大利人跟过来看，站着瞪。弗莱厄蒂挥手赶他们，像轰鸡一样。
「走开。」他说；「管你们自己的事去。」
他们慢慢走开，一步三回头。
「行了，」里科说，「有什么话就说。」
弗莱厄蒂掏出一支大雪茄，嚼了起来。另一个人还在盯着看。里科纳闷，不知玩的什么名堂；随后他注意到走廊这一头光线很好，比走廊里任何地方都好。仔细打量？嗯，那又怎样？

===Original===
“Listen, Rico,” said Flaherty, “I like you and I’m going to give you a tip. It’s going to be tough on you birds from now on. The Old Man’s got his back up. Now get this. If you got anything on your mind, you better spill it.” Flaherty paused to light his cigar. The other detective watched Rico intently. “Because it’s going to be easy for the bird that spilled it first. But God help the rest of them.”
Rico smiled slightly.
“Quit stalling,” he said.
Flaherty glanced at the man with him, but the man shook his head. Flaherty said:
“Well, I’m giving you a friendly tip.”
“Yeah,” said Rico, “you bulls always was friendly as hell. I spent two years once just thinking how friendly you was. Listen, I ain’t got nothing to spill. What the hell’s wrong with you, Flaherty? Did I ever do any spilling?”
===Chinese===
「听着，里科，」弗莱厄蒂说，「我看得起你，给你透个信。从今往后，你们这帮鸟人的日子要难过了。老头子动怒了。记住。你要是心里有什么，最好吐出来。」弗莱厄蒂停下来点雪茄。另一个警探目不转睛地看着里科。「因为先开口的那只鸟日子好过。其余的，就只有求上帝保佑了。」
里科淡淡一笑。
「别兜圈子了。」他说。
弗莱厄蒂看了眼身边的人，那人摇了摇头。弗莱厄蒂说：
「唉，我这是给你一个善意的提醒。」
「是啊，」里科说，「你们这些条子向来他妈友好得很。有一回我蹲了两年，天天琢磨你们有多友好。听着，我没什么可吐的。你到底怎么回事，弗莱厄蒂？我什么时候漏过什么？」

===Original===
Flaherty laughed.
“Well,” he said, “there’s a first time for everything. All right, Rico, you can go.”
The two plainclothes men pushed their way through the crowd and went down the stairs. Rico went back into Tony’s flat. Sam Vettori and Otero were waiting for him. Vettori was mopping his face with his big, white silk handkerchief.
“Well?” he demanded. Rico shrugged.
“Just stalling.”
“What’s the name?”
===Chinese===
弗莱厄蒂笑了。
「唉，」他说，「什么事都有头一回。行，里科，你可以走了。」
两个便衣挤过人群，下了楼。里科回到托尼的公寓。萨姆·维托里和奥特罗在等他。维托里正用那块白绸大手帕擦脸。
「怎么样？」他问。里科耸耸肩。
「就是兜圈子。」
「打的什么主意？」

===Original===
“You got me. I guess Flaherty wanted this other bird to give me the once-over.”
“Things getting pretty hot, Rico.”
“Don’t beef, Sam. We’re gonna come through.”
Otero said:
“The old lady sure is taking it hard.”
They could hear Tony’s mother sobbing loudly in the next room.
===Chinese===
「猜不透。我看是弗莱厄蒂想让另外那个家伙把我相个仔细。」
「事情越来越烫了，里科。」
「别抱怨了，萨姆。咱们会挺过去的。」
奥特罗说：
「老太太可真伤心。」
他们听得见托尼的母亲在隔壁放声痛哭。

## Part IV / 第四部

### I / 一

===Original===
For three or four years Bat Carillo, once a third-rate light-heavyweight, had been the leader of one of Vettori’s gangs of hooligans. The members of this gang specialized in strong arm stuff and intimidation; they threw bombs; they smashed up barrooms and vice-joints operated by rival gangs. They were, in other words, Vettori’s shock troops. Carillo was an excellent lieutenant, as he always carried out orders to the letter; and was congenitally incapable of imagining himself as chief in his own right. A good, honest subordinate without ambition. Vettori trusted him.
In Carillo’s attitude since the killing of Courtney, therefore, Vettori saw the most unmistakable symptom of his own passing. Carillo had attached himself to Rico and called him “boss.” Carillo was not careless with the word “boss”; it was not a conventional expression; when he said “boss” he meant it. Aroused, Vettori saw similar manifestations all around him; in Blackie Avezzano, in Killer Pepi, in a dozen others.
Vettori had always disliked Rico. Now he hated him. If Carillo or Killer Pepi had remained faithful, he would have had one of them kill him and damn the consequences. But there was no question of that now. He knew that he was whipped and he saw the necessity of a compromise. Hanging was just over the horizon, and Rico’s gun promised an even more certain death. Vettori had never split with anyone. He had always taken with both hands and given as little as possible. But it was split now or die, and Vettori could not contemplate the prospect of dying with any degree of complacency. He sent for Rico.
===Chinese===
巴特·卡里洛从前是个三流轻重量级拳手，三四年来一直替维托里管着一帮流氓。这帮人专干暴力行凶和恐吓的营生：扔炸弹，砸对头帮派开的酒吧和黄赌窝点。换句话说，他们是维托里的突击队。卡里洛是个顶好的副手，执行命令从来一字不差，而且天生想不出自己另立山头当头儿这码事。一个规规矩矩、忠心耿耿、没有野心的下属。维托里信任他。
因此，自从考特尼（Courtney）被杀之后，卡里洛的态度在维托里眼里就成了自己即将完蛋的最明白不过的征兆。卡里洛已经贴到了里科身边，管他叫「老大」。卡里洛说「老大」二字从不随便；那不是一句应酬话；他喊「老大」的时候，是当真的。一经警觉，维托里便在四周看到了同样的迹象：布莱基·阿韦扎诺身上，杀手佩皮身上，还有十来个别人身上。
维托里从来就不喜欢里科。如今他恨他。倘若卡里洛或者杀手佩皮还忠心，他随便点一个去干掉里科，后果去他妈的。可如今没这个指望了。他知道自己败局已定，也看出必须妥协。绞架已在不远处候着，而里科的枪许下的死还要更稳当。维托里从来没跟人分过家。他向来双手往里捞，往外掏得越少越好。可这回不分手就得死，而维托里一想到死，实在没法心安。他派人去叫里科。

===Original===
A new Rico appeared, followed by Otero, Carillo, and Killer Pepi. Rico was wearing a big ulster like Joe’s and a derby also like Joe’s. He had on fawn-coloured spats drawn over pointed patent-leather shoes; and a diamond horseshoe pin sparkled in a red, green and white striped necktie.
Vettori looked him over and winked at Killer Pepi, but Killer Pepi’s face was stony. Carillo got a chair for Rico.
“What’s on your mind, Sam?” said Rico, sitting down, throwing back his ulster and pulling up his trousers to preserve the crease.
Vettori hesitated.
“I want to see you alone,” he said.
===Chinese===
里科一副新派头出现了，身后跟着奥特罗、卡里洛和杀手佩皮。他穿一件乔那种款式的长大衣，戴一顶乔那种圆顶硬礼帽。尖头漆皮鞋外面罩着浅黄色鞋罩，红绿白三色条纹领带上，一枚马蹄形钻石别针闪闪发亮。
维托里上下打量他，朝杀手佩皮递了个眼色，佩皮却面无表情。卡里洛给里科搬来椅子。
「有什么事，萨姆？」里科说着坐下，敞开大衣，提起裤脚护住裤线。
维托里迟疑了一下。
「我想单独跟你谈谈。」他说。

===Original===
“No,” said Rico, “I think I know your game, Sam, and I want the boys to get an earful. Go ahead and spill it.”
Vettori began to sweat. Killer Pepi said:
“Yeah, we know.”
“You know a hell of a lot, don’t you?” said Vettori.
“We know, all right,” said Pepi.
Nobody said anything. Rico took off his hat and began to comb his hair. Vettori got out his cards and began to lay out a game of solitaire.
===Chinese===
「不行，」里科说，「你那点算盘我大概齐知道，萨姆，我要让弟兄们都听听。有话就往外倒吧。」
维托里开始冒汗。杀手佩皮说：
「没错，我们知道。」
「你们知道得可真他妈多，是不是？」维托里说。
「我们知道，错不了。」佩皮说。
谁也没吭声。里科摘下帽子，梳起头来。维托里掏出纸牌，摆起了单人牌局。

===Original===
Pepi said:
“We know you went yellow, Sam, when Tony blew his top and started after Come-To-Jesus McConagha. We know all right.”
Vettori looked up at him.
“What the hell I got you guys for anyway! Who hands out the cush?”
Rico paused in the combing of his hair.
“Don’t get rough, Sam.”
===Chinese===
佩皮说：
「我们知道你怂了，萨姆——托尼火冒三丈、追着『来见耶稣』麦科纳加（McConagha）干的时候，你怂了。我们全知道。」
维托里抬眼看他。
「不然老子养你们这帮家伙干什么！油水是谁发的？」
里科梳头的手停了停。
「别来横的，萨姆。」

===Original===
Killer Pepi went over and stood with his back against the door. Otero sat down opposite Vettori.
“Well,” said Rico, “if you want to see me, spill it quick because I ain’t got all night.”
Vettori sighed profoundly, then he put down his cards and looked at the men around him. He saw four hostile faces.
“All right,” said Vettori, “but why the strong arm stuff, Rico? Sit down, you guys, and I’ll have some drinks sent up.”
The three men looked at Rico.
“All right,” he said, “go bring up some drink, Bat.”
===Chinese===
杀手佩皮走过去，背抵着门站定。奥特罗在维托里对面坐下。
「行了，」里科说，「要找我谈就快点摊牌，我可没一整夜的工夫。」
维托里长叹一声，放下牌，看了看周围的人。他看到四张满怀敌意的脸。
「好好，」维托里说，「可干吗摆这全副武行的阵仗，里科？都坐吧，弟兄们，我叫人送些喝的上来。」
三个人都望着里科。
「行，」他说，「去拿点喝的上来，巴特。」

===Original===
Carillo went out. Nobody said anything. Outside, a winter dusk settled and the big electric sign on a level with the windows was switched on.
Carillo brought in the drinks and they all sat around the table under the green-shaded lamp. Otero, Carillo, and Killer Pepi drank whisky; Vettori wine; Rico pop.
Vettori put down his glass.
“Well, Rico,” he said, “I got a proposition to make you.”
“All right,” said Rico, “spring it.”
“Listen,” said Vettori, “I’m getting old. I’ll never see forty-five again, and when a guy’s that old he ain’t worth much.”
===Chinese===
卡里洛出去了。谁也没说话。外面，冬日的暮色四合，与窗子齐平的那块大电招牌亮了起来。
卡里洛把酒端进来，众人围着绿罩台灯下的桌子坐下。奥特罗、卡里洛和杀手佩皮喝威士忌；维托里喝葡萄酒；里科喝汽水。
维托里放下杯子。
「是这样，里科，」他说，「我有个提议要跟你谈。」
「行，」里科说，「放马过来。」
「听着，」维托里说，「我老了。四十五岁再也回不去了，人活到这份儿上就不值几个钱了。」

===Original===
“You ain’t getting old, Sam, you’re losing your guts,” said Rico. Killer Pepi laughed out aloud and banged his fist on the table. But Vettori swallowed this insult.
“All right, Rico,” he said, “that’s your story. Well, here’s how it is. I need a partner. You’re young, Rico, and you got the guts. All the guys like you and they’ll do what you say. I got the layout and you’re looking for a chance to be a big guy. Well, here’s your chance.” Vettori thought for a moment, then he said: “I’ll split the works with you.”
Carillo and Pepi exchanged a look. Otero began to hum to himself. But Rico said:
“I’ll think it over.”
Vettori began to sweat again. Was Rico going to get rid of him?
“Well,” he said, putting on a front, “you can take it or leave it. I like you, Rico, and I’m doing you a favour. Who’s got the money? Who’s got the pull? What the hell would you guys do if you didn’t have the Big Boy to pull you through?”
===Chinese===
「你不是老了，萨姆，你是胆气散了。」里科说。杀手佩皮放声大笑，拳头砸在桌上。维托里把这口气咽了下去。
「好，里科，」他说，「那是你的说法。事情是这么回事：我需要一个搭档。你年轻，里科，有胆量。弟兄们都喜欢你，你说什么他们干什么。盘子在我手里，而你正寻摸机会当大人物。喏，机会来了。」维托里想了想，接着说：「这摊买卖，跟你对半分。」
卡里洛和佩皮交换了一个眼色。奥特罗自顾自哼起歌来。里科却说：
「我考虑考虑。」
维托里又冒汗了。里科是要除掉他？
「哼，」他硬撑着场面说，「要不要随你的便。我喜欢你，里科，我这是抬举你。钱在谁手里？门路在谁手里？没有大老板替你们兜着，你们这帮家伙顶什么用？」

===Original===
“I’m OK with the Big Boy,” said Rico; “he was up to see me this morning.”
“Yeah,” said Pepi, “I brung him.”
Vettori laid out a new game of solitaire.
“Here’s the thing,” said Rico: “you’re trying to hang on, Sam. You must think we’re dumb as hell. You want me to do the work so you can take it easy. And you call that an even split. Hear what I say! That ain’t my idea of a split.”
“Well, I ain’t handing out charity,” said Vettori, losing his temper.
Rico got to his feet and buttoned up his ulster.
===Chinese===
「我跟大老板处得挺好，」里科说，「他今儿早上还上来看过我。」
「没错，」佩皮说，「是我带他来的。」
维托里重新摆了一局单人牌。
「事情是这样的，」里科说，「你想死耗着不撒手，萨姆。你准是当我们傻透了。活儿我干，清福你享，你还管这叫对半分。听我一句！这可不是我要的分法。」
「哼，我这儿可不办善堂。」维托里说，按捺不住发起火来。
里科站起身，扣上大衣的扣子。

===Original===
“All right, Sam.”
Vettori slammed down his cards.
“What do you guys think?” he demanded of Carillo, Pepi and Otero.
“Ain’t that a fair split?”
They just looked at him.
“No,” said Rico, “I guess we can’t do no business.”
===Chinese===
「就这样，萨姆。」
维托里把牌往桌上一摔。
「你们几个怎么看？」他冲卡里洛、佩皮和奥特罗质问。
「这分法还不公道吗？」
他们只是看着他。
「不公道，」里科说，「我看这买卖是谈不成了。」

===Original===
Rico put on his hat and walked toward the door. The other three got up and followed him. Vettori stood up.
“Well,” he said, “you gonna try to run me out, Rico?”
Vettori was panicky. Rico stood at the door and looked at him.
“I was just figuring I’d open a joint across the street,” he said.
Vettori knew what he meant. He had been through half a dozen gang wars, but that was long ago when there were at least five separate gangs in the neighbourhood. Things had been comparatively quiet for over three years. Vettori regretted the past bitterly. He regretted having taken up with Rico, an unknown Youngstown wop.
“Well,” he said, “Rico, you’re young and you ain’t got any too much sense. What the hell! With things the way they are, we wouldn’t none of us last a month. Listen, Rico, what’s your idea of a split?”
===Chinese===
里科戴上帽子，朝门口走去。另外三人起身跟上。维托里站了起来。
「怎么着，」他说，「你是想把我挤走，里科？」
维托里心里发慌。里科站在门口，望着他。
「我正琢磨着，在街对面开一家场子。」他说。
维托里明白这话的意思。帮派火并他身经五六场，可那是老早的事了，那会儿这一带至少有五拨各立旗号的帮派。三年多来，世面还算太平。维托里把过去恨得咬牙。他悔就悔在当初收留了里科，扬斯敦（Youngstown）来的一个无名意大利佬。
「好吧，」他说，「里科，你年轻，脑子也不太够使。去他妈的！照眼下这个世道，咱们谁也活不过一个月。听着，里科，你说怎么分？」

===Original===
Rico took off his hat and scratched his head, but carefully so that his hair wouldn’t be disarranged.
“I’ll hand you this, Sam,” said Rico, “you got the layout. The split’s good that way. But you got sense enough to know that no two guys can run things. The layout split is OK with me, but I got to have the say, get that!”
Vettori looked at the others.
“What do you guys say?”
“We’re in with Rico,” said Killer Pepi.
Otero and Carillo nodded. Vettori brought his hand down on the table with a smack.
===Chinese===
里科摘下帽子挠了挠头，下手很小心，没弄乱头发。
「给你句实话，萨姆，」里科说，「盘子是在你手里。这么分，行。可你不至于蠢到不知道一山不容二虎。盘子照分，但说话算数的得是我，记住！」
维托里看看其余几人。
「你们几个怎么说？」
「我们跟里科。」杀手佩皮说。
奥特罗和卡里洛点了点头。维托里一巴掌拍在桌上。

===Original===
“OK,” he said.
===Chinese===
「行。」他说。

### II / 二

===Original===
The gang gave a banquet for Rico in one of Sam Vettori’s big back rooms. The table was fifteen feet long and was covered by a fine white cloth. Red, green and white streamers hung from the chandeliers and Italian and American flags were crossed at intervals along the walls. At eleven o’clock the notables began to arrive. Killer Pepi in a blue suit and a brown derby, with his woman, Blue Jay, on his arm. Joe Sansone, gunman and ex-lightweight, in a tuxedo, followed by his shadow Kid Bean, a Sicilian, dark as a negro. Then Ottavio Vettori, Sam’s cousin, not yet twenty-one, already famous as a gunman and spoken of as a potential gang chief. Then Otero, Blackie Avezzano and Bat Carillo, all with their women. They stood about stiffly, a little uncomfortable in their fine clothes, and tried to make conversation. The men, like all specialists, talked shop. Ottavio Vettori declared that the police were a bunch of bums. Killer Pepi agreed that they were. Joe Sansone said that the Federal men were just as bad, only smarter and crookeder. Killer Pepi agreed that they were. Ottavio Vettori didn’t agree. He said that the Federal men were dumber and harder to fix. This brought on an argument.
When Sam Vettori came in the men were all shouting.
“What the hell!” said Sam, “ain’t this a fine way to act at a banquet? You act like a bunch of gas house micks. Cut the chatter.”
Ottavio made a noise like a goat.
“Baa! Baa!”
Everybody laughed. Otero took out a quart bottle of whiskey, drank from it and passed it to Seal Skin; she drank and passed it to Ottavio. The bottle circled the room and returned empty.
===Chinese===
帮里在萨姆·维托里一间大里屋为里科摆了宴席。桌子足有十五英尺长，铺着雪白的细桌布。红绿白三色彩带从吊灯上垂挂下来，墙上每隔一段便交叉着挂一面意大利国旗和一面美国国旗。十一点钟，头面人物陆续到场。杀手佩皮一身蓝西装配棕色圆顶礼帽，胳膊上挎着他的女人蓝松鸦（Blue Jay）。乔·桑松，枪手兼前轻量级拳手，一身燕尾服，身后跟着他的影子小比恩——一个西西里人，黑得像个黑人。接着是奥塔维奥·维托里（Ottavio Vettori），萨姆的堂弟，还不到二十一岁，已经以枪手闻名，被人念叨着是将来帮里头儿的料。再接着是奥特罗、布莱基·阿韦扎诺和巴特·卡里洛，都带着各自的女人。他们僵着身子四处站着，好衣服穿在身上有点不自在，没话找话。男人们跟所有行家一样，三句话不离本行。奥塔维奥·维托里宣称警察是一帮废物。杀手佩皮附和说就是。乔·桑松说联邦探员也一样操蛋，只是更精也更坏。杀手佩皮附和说就是。奥塔维奥·维托里这回不附和了。他说联邦探员更蠢，也更难打点。这下吵起来了。
萨姆·维托里进来时，众人正吵作一团。
「见鬼！」萨姆说，「宴会上就这么个德行？瞧你们跟一帮煤气厂爱尔兰粗坯似的。都闭嘴。」
奥塔维奥学山羊叫了起来。
「咩！咩！」
众人大笑。奥特罗掏出一夸脱瓶装的威士忌，对着嘴灌了一口，递给海豹皮；她喝了，传给奥塔维奥。酒瓶绕屋一圈，传回来时已经空了。

===Original===
“You sure came prepared, you birds,” said Sam. “Did any of you guys bring a lunch?”
“Baa! Baa!” bellowed Ottavio.
“My God, ain’t that cute!” said Killer Pepi’s girl.
“Hell, that ain’t nothing,” said Pepi, “listen.” Pepi put three fingers in his mouth and blew a blast that made their eardrums ring.
“Lord,” said Ottavio, “the cops! Baa! Baa!”
Three waiters came in, each carrying two quarts of whiskey. They put the bottles on the table and went out.
===Chinese===
「你们这帮家伙可真是有备而来，」萨姆说，「谁带吃的来了没有？」
「咩！咩！」奥塔维奥扯着嗓子吼。
「我的天，真逗！」杀手佩皮的女人说。
「嗐，这算什么，」佩皮说，「听这个。」佩皮把三根手指塞进嘴里，吹出一声唿哨，震得众人耳膜嗡嗡响。
「老天，」奥塔维奥说，「条子来了！咩！咩！」
三个侍者进来，每人端着两夸脱威士忌。他们把酒瓶放上桌，又出去了。

===Original===
“That’s an appetizer,” said Sam.
“Apéritif,” Joe Sansone corrected.
Ottavio slapped him on the back.
“What’s that, little Joe? What the hell lingo is that?”
Joe pushed him away.
“You dumb birds don’t know nothing. Swell people don’t say ‘appetizer’; they say ’apéritif.’ ”
===Chinese===
「这是开胃酒。」萨姆说。
「阿佩里蒂夫。」乔·桑松纠正道。
奥塔维奥拍了拍他的背。
「说的什么呀，小乔？这他妈是哪国鸟语？」
乔把他推开。
「你们这帮蠢鸟什么都不懂。上等人不说『开胃酒』，人家说『阿佩里蒂夫』。」

===Original===
“The hell they do! Well, I expect you know all about it. You used to be a bellboy at the Blackstone.”
Everybody laughed. Killer Pepi blew a blast on his fingers. His girl looked at him admiringly.
“How the hell you ever learn to do that?”
“Aw, that ain’t nothing.”
“Say, Sam,” said Carillo, “when do we eat?”
“When the boss gets here,” said Pepi.
===Chinese===
「鬼才这么说！得了，想必你门儿清。你从前可是布莱克斯通饭店（Blackstone）的门童。」
众人大笑。杀手佩皮又吹了一声指哨。他的女人一脸崇拜地望着他。
「你他妈这是怎么学会的？」
「嗐，这算个屁。」
「哎，萨姆，」卡里洛说，「什么时候开饭？」
「等老大到了再说。」佩皮说。

===Original===
“Well, he better step on it because I’m so hungry I could eat dynamite,” said Ottavio.
“Keep your shirt on,” said Pepi.
“Haven’t got an old soup sandwich in your pocket, have you?” asked Ottavio.
Everybody laughed. Ottavio was the recognized wit of the Vettori gang. All that he had to do to get a laugh was to open his mouth.
Sam Vettori took one of the quarts from the table and sent it round the room. It came back empty.
“What the hell you suppose is keeping Rico?” asked Carillo. “Keep your shirt on,” said Pepi.
===Chinese===
「那他最好麻利点儿，我饿得都能吞炸药了。」奥塔维奥说。
「别急，沉住气。」佩皮说。
「你该不会兜里揣着块烂糊糊的汤三明治吧？」奥塔维奥问。
众人大笑。奥塔维奥是维托里帮里公认的活宝。他只要一张嘴，就有笑料。
萨姆·维托里从桌上拿起一瓶夸脱装，让它在屋里传了一圈。传回来时又空了。
「你说里科他妈的怎么还不来？」卡里洛问。「别急，沉住气。」佩皮说。

===Original===
“I go see,” said Otero.
As he went out, the Big Boy came in. He had on a big racoon coat and his derby was on the side of his head. Sam Vettori rushed over and shook hands with him.
“What the hell you doing here?” he demanded.
“Me. I came to see the fun. Things are looking up, Sam. Things sure to God are looking up. I think we got ’em whipped.”
Sam Vettori smiled broadly and poured the Big Boy a drink. Well, well! If the Courtney business blew over he was sitting pretty. All things considered, he hadn’t done so bad. Time after time he had seen old gang leaders go down before younger men. But here he was hanging on, getting a 50﻿–﻿50 split, and taking no chances. Rico was the goods. Goddamn him and all his kind, but he was the goods.
“Yeah,” said the Big Boy, “you got the Old Man on the run and Flaherty’s about ready to do the Dutch Act. It’s gonna blow over, Sam. You heard me speak. It’s gonna blow over. I want to see Rico.”
===Chinese===
「我去看看。」奥特罗说。
他刚出去，大老板走了进来。他裹着一件大浣熊皮大衣，圆顶礼帽歪戴在脑袋上。萨姆·维托里赶紧迎上去同他握手。
「你他妈怎么跑这儿来了？」他问。
「我啊，来瞧瞧热闹。世道在好转，萨姆。老天作证，世道真在好转。我看咱们把他们治服了。」
萨姆·维托里堆起满脸笑，给大老板斟了一杯。好啊，好啊！只要考特尼那桩事就这么平下去，他的位子就坐得四平八稳。统共算下来，他干得不算赖。老帮派头目栽在年轻人手里，这种戏码他见了一回又一回。可他还稳稳当当挂着帅印，拿着 50 对 50 的分账，一点风险不沾。里科是块好料。叫他和他那号人统统见鬼去吧，可他确实是块好料。
「是啊，」大老板说，「老头子被你逼得团团转，弗莱厄蒂（Flaherty）也差不多要撂挑子跑路了。这事会平过去的，萨姆。我的话你听见了。会平过去的。我要见里科。」

===Original===
“He ain’t showed yet,” said Sam.
“Damn smart boy,” said the Big Boy.
Sam smiled.
“Yeah,” he said, pouring the Big Boy another drink, “damn smart kid. He’s young yet, but I can show him the ropes.”
The Big Boy didn’t say anything. He just looked at Vettori. Otero came running in, followed by two waiters, one of whom was carrying a big ulster and a derby; the other was carrying a woman’s fur coat.
“Here he comes,” cried Otero.
===Chinese===
「他还没露面呢。」萨姆说。
「精得没边的小子。」大老板说。
萨姆笑了笑。
「是啊，」他一边给大老板再斟一杯一边说，「精得没边的小子。他还嫩，不过路子我可以带他。」
大老板没接话，只看着维托里。奥特罗跑着进来，身后跟着两个侍者，一个捧着件长大衣和一顶圆顶礼帽，另一个捧着件女人的皮草。
「他来了。」奥特罗喊道。

===Original===
Kid Bean, who had collected a crowd in the middle of the room, and was walking on his hands to amuse them (he had once been an acrobat), jumped hastily to his feet and backed up against the wall. The crowd followed him. Killer Pepi said:
“All right now. Everybody yell like hell when he comes in.” Rico came in slowly, talking to Blondy Belle, the swellest woman in Little Italy. She was a handsome Italian, bold and aquiline. Her complexion and eyes were dark, but her hair, naturally black, was blondined, and this gave her an incongruous and a somewhat formidable appearance.
Rico was greeted by an uproar, pierced by Killer Pepi’s shrill whistle. The Big Boy went to meet Rico and shook hands with him. Sam Vettori smiled and nodded, very affably, then went out to get things started. The Big Boy said to Blondy Belle:
“Got yourself a regular man, did you?”
Blondy took hold of Rico’s arm.
“Surest thing you know.”
===Chinese===
小比恩本来在屋子当中聚起一圈人，正拿大顶逗他们乐（他从前当过杂技演员），一听这话急忙翻身站起，退到墙边。那圈人跟着他退了过去。杀手佩皮说：
「都听好了。他一进门，都给我往死里喊。」里科缓步走了进来，同布朗迪·贝尔说着话——小意大利最出众的女人。她是个标致的意大利女人，眉眼放肆，鼻梁如鹰钩。肤色和眼睛都是深色的，可天生黑发染成了金黄色，这给她添了一股说不搭调、又颇有几分唬人的气派。
一阵喧嚣迎接里科，杀手佩皮尖利的唿哨刺在里头。大老板迎上去同里科握手。萨姆·维托里满面和气地笑着点头，随即出去张罗开席。大老板对布朗迪·贝尔说：
「给自己找了条真汉子，是吧？」
布朗迪挽住里科的胳膊。
「这还用说。」

===Original===
The Big Boy laughed.
“What’d you do with Little Arnie?”
Rico took out a cigar and bit off the end.
“She ditched him,” he said.
The Big Boy meditated. Blondy Belle had been Little Arnie’s woman for a long time. Little Arnie ran the biggest gambling joint on the North Side, but he had been slipping for a year or more. He wasn’t right; nobody could trust him.
“How did Little Arnie take it?” asked the Big Boy.
===Chinese===
大老板哈哈一笑。
「那小阿尼你怎么打发的？」
里科掏出一支雪茄，咬掉了头。
「她把他甩了。」他说。
大老板沉吟着。布朗迪·贝尔做小阿尼的女人做了很久。小阿尼开着北区最大的赌场，可一年多来一直在走下坡。他不对劲；谁也信不过他。
「小阿尼咽下这口气了？」大老板问。

===Original===
“He took it standing up,” said Blondy Belle.
“Well, what could he do?” said Rico.
Killer Pepi, Ottavio Vettori and Joe Sansone, as the most important men in the gang next to Sam Vettori, came over to shake hands with Rico.
“A million dollars ain’t in it with you,” said Pepi, looking his boss over.
Rico was wearing a loud striped suit and a purple tie. He still had on his gloves, yellow kid, of which he was very proud, and his diamond horseshoe pin had been replaced by a big ruby surrounded by little diamonds. Ottavio envied him his gloves. But Joe Sansone was not impressed; he knew better.
“Yes sir, boss, you sure are lit up,” said Ottavio.
===Chinese===
「他站着受下的。」布朗迪·贝尔说。
「唉，他还能怎么着？」里科说。
杀手佩皮、奥塔维奥·维托里和乔·桑松，作为帮里仅次于萨姆·维托里的要人，走过来同里科握手。
「跟您这行头比，一百万都不算回事。」佩皮说着，把自家老大上下打量了一番。
里科穿着一身扎眼的条纹西服，系一条紫领带。手上还戴着那副黄羊皮手套——他宝贝得很——领带上那枚马蹄形钻石别针换成了一颗大红宝石，四周围着一圈小钻。奥塔维奥眼馋他那副手套。乔·桑松却不买账；他心里有数。
「可不是嘛，老大，您今儿晚上真是浑身放光。」奥塔维奥说。

===Original===
“Here’s the half-pint,” said Killer Pepi, pushing Joe Sansone forward.
Joe shook hands with Rico.
“Yes sir,” said Ottavio, “the half-pint’s a good boy, but he and Gentleman Joe’re too swell for us.”
Rico looked around the room.
“Joe Massara here?”
“Ain’t seen him,” said Pepi.
===Chinese===
「这位是小不点儿。」杀手佩皮说着，把乔·桑松推上前。
乔同里科握了手。
「可不是嘛，」奥塔维奥说，「小不点儿是条好汉，就是他跟绅士乔（Gentleman Joe）俩太把自己当上等人，跟我们不是一路。」
里科环视屋子。
「乔·马萨拉来了吗？」
「没瞧见。」佩皮说。

===Original===
“He won’t be here,” said Joe Sansone; “he’s busy.”
Rico didn’t say anything. Blondy took hold of his arm.
“I want a drink.”
Rico looked at Pepi.
“Get her a drink,” he said.
The Big Boy took Rico aside and said:
===Chinese===
「他不会来了，」乔·桑松说，「他忙着呢。」
里科没作声。布朗迪挽住他的胳膊。
「我要喝一杯。」
里科看向佩皮。
「给她拿杯喝的。」他说。
大老板把里科拉到一边，说：

===Original===
“I want to see you a minute, Rico.”
Rico said:
“Listen, if you see Joe Massara tomorrow you tell him to look me up. I got something to say to that bird.”
“I’ll be seeing him maybe,” said the Big Boy. “I got a date with his boss tomorrow morning. There’s a square guy, Rico. DeVoss is a square guy all right. Never have to nudge him for dough.” Rico seemed in a bad humour.
“They tell me you lined up something good,” said the Big Boy. Rico nodded.
“Yeah, it’s gonna be a money maker. Little Arnie wised me up. I’m gonna give him a split. That’s the game now. Sam never had sense enough to get in on it.”
===Chinese===
「我有话跟你说，里科，就一分钟。」
里科说：
「听着，你明天要是见着乔·马萨拉，叫他来找我。我有话要跟那家伙讲。」
「兴许会见着，」大老板说，「明早我约了他老板。那是个痛快人，里科。德沃斯（DeVoss）是个痛快人，掏钱从来不等人开口。」里科像是一肚子不痛快。
「听说你张罗上了一票好买卖。」大老板说。里科点头。
「嗯，是个来钱的营生。小阿尼给我递的点子。我要分他一份。如今兴这个。萨姆脑子不够，一直没赶上趟。」

===Original===
“Little Arnie, eh? That guy’d double-cross his grandmother.”
“He’ll only double-cross me once,” said Rico.
“I believe you,” said the Big Boy; then, putting his hand on Rico’s shoulder, he went on: “Funny for you to split with Arnie. How about Blondy?”
“Arnie don’t give a damn. He’s all shot to pieces. He can’t do a woman no good.”
“No wonder,” said the Big Boy, “with a woman like that.” Rico grinned.
“Ain’t she a bearcat!” he said; then his face clouded. “Wonder what the hell Joe Massara’s game is?”
===Chinese===
「小阿尼，嗯？那家伙连他亲奶奶都敢卖。」
「他卖我，也就卖得着一回。」里科说。
「这话我信，」大老板说；接着把手搭在里科肩上，继续道：「你跟阿尼合伙倒是新鲜。布朗迪怎么办？」
「阿尼才不在乎呢。他整个人垮了。他对女人已经没用了。」
「难怪，」大老板说，「摊上那么个女人。」里科咧嘴一笑。
「她是不是个辣货！」他说；随即脸色阴了下来。「真不知道乔·马萨拉他妈在搞什么名堂？」

===Original===
The Big Boy looked at Rico for a moment.
“That little hunky dancer over at DeVoss’s has got him down. They tell me he’s going straight.”
Rico laughed unpleasantly.
“Yeah? Well, I’ll have to go over and give that bird an earful.”
“Better stay out of that end of town, Rico.”
“To hell with that.”
===Chinese===
大老板盯着里科看了一会儿。
「德沃斯那边那个东欧小舞娘把他拿捏住了。听说他要走正道了。」
里科笑得难听。
「是吗？行啊，那我还真得过去一趟，好好说道说道那家伙。」
「城那头你最好别去，里科。」
「去他妈的。」

===Original===
Sam Vettori came in, followed by three waiters bringing the soup.
“All right,” said Sam, “we’re all set.”
Rico took his place at the head of the table. The Big Boy sat on his right and Blondy Belle on his left. The gunmen and their women arranged themselves according to rank. Blackie Avezzano sat at the foot of the table.
===Chinese===
萨姆·维托里进来，身后三个侍者端着汤。
「好了，」萨姆说，「都齐了。」
里科在主位落座。大老板坐他右手，布朗迪·贝尔坐他左手。枪手们带着各自的女人，按地位高低依次入座。布莱基·阿韦扎诺坐了末席。

### III / 三

===Original===
When the meal was over, the Big Boy asked Rico to make a speech. There was a prolonged clamour. Rico got up.
“All right,” he said, “if you birds want me to make a speech, here you are: I want to thank you guys for this banquet. It sure is swell. The liquor is good, so they tell me, I don’t drink it myself, and the food don’t leave nothing to be desired. I guess we all had a swell time and it sure is good to see all you guys gathered together. Well, I guess that’s about all. Only I wish you guys wouldn’t get drunk and raise hell, as that’s the way a lot of birds get bumped off.”
Rico sat down. The applause lasted for over a minute. Then Ottavio got up with a bottle in his hand.
“Here’s to Rico and Blondy and the Big Boy.”
Everybody shouted and made a grab for bottles and glasses. Blackie Avezzano fell under the table and stayed there, lying on his face. After the toast was drunk, Killer Pepi and Kid Bean began to quarrel. The Kid picked up a plate and struck at Pepi, who threw a bottle at the Kid, missing him by a fraction of an inch.
Rico banged on the table.
===Chinese===
宴席吃罢，大老板请里科讲几句。满堂起哄了好一阵。里科站起身。
「行，」他说，「你们这帮家伙既然要我讲话，那就听着：这场宴席，我谢谢诸位。场面是真阔。酒是好酒——听人说的，我自己不喝——饭菜也没得挑。我看大家吃得都尽兴，你们这帮人能聚在一块儿，瞧着是真叫人高兴。行了，我要说的就这些。只盼各位别喝多了撒酒疯，多少条好汉就是栽在这上头的。」
里科坐下。掌声持续了一分多钟。接着奥塔维奥手里拎着酒瓶站了起来。
「敬里科，敬布朗迪，敬大老板！」
众人哄然叫好，纷纷去抓瓶子和杯子。布莱基·阿韦扎诺栽到桌子底下，脸朝下趴着不起来了。祝酒喝过，杀手佩皮和小比恩拌起嘴来。小比恩抓起盘子朝佩皮劈去，佩皮抡起酒瓶砸那小子，只差不到一寸就砸中了。
里科砰砰敲桌子。

===Original===
“Cut it out, you guys. Ain’t that a hell of a way to act?”
Pepi and the Kid shook hands and another toast was drunk.
A waiter came in the door and went over to Rico.
“Couple of newspaper guys, boss. They want to take a flashlight.”
“What’s the idea?” the Big Boy inquired.
“Send ’em up,” said Rico.
===Chinese===
「都住手。这算什么德行？」
佩皮和小比恩握了握手，又干了一轮。
一个侍者进门，走到里科跟前。
「来了两个报馆的，老大。他们想拍张镁光灯照。」
「想干什么？」大老板问。
「让他们上来。」里科说。

===Original===
“We’re gonna get our mugs shot,” cried Blondy Belle.
“Maybe we are,” said Rico.
“What’s the idea?” the Big Boy reiterated.
“We ain’t got nothing to hide,” said Rico.
The waiter returned, followed by two newspaper men, one of whom was carrying a big camera. Rico motioned them over.
“Who sent you?” he asked.
===Chinese===
「咱们要上报照相啦。」布朗迪·贝尔嚷道。
「也许吧。」里科说。
「到底想干什么？」大老板又问。
「咱们没什么见不得人的。」里科说。
侍者回来了，身后跟着两个报馆的人，其中一个扛着台大相机。里科招手让他们过来。
「谁派你们来的？」他问。

===Original===
Sam Vettori came in and went over to Rico.
“They’re OK, Rico,” he said, “they been here before.”
“Sure, we’re OK,” said the photographer, a little intimidated by Rico’s manner.
“Well, spill it,” said Rico, “what’s the idea of the flashlight?”
“Well, we got a section in the Sunday paper about how different classes of people live in Chicago. See? Last week we featured Lake Forest. Had some pictures of the swells, see, and the dumps where they lived. This Sunday we want Little Italy. We just heard about the banquet they were giving you, Mr. Rico, so we kinda thought﻿ ﻿…”
“OK,” said Rico, “but make it snappy.”
===Chinese===
萨姆·维托里进来，走到里科身边。
「他们没问题，里科，」他说，「以前来过。」
「真没问题，」摄影记者说，让里科的做派镇得有点发怵。
「行，说吧，」里科说，「拍这镁光灯是什么意思？」
「是这样，礼拜天的报纸有个版面，讲芝加哥各阶层的人怎么过日子。明白吗？上礼拜我们做的是莱克福里斯特（Lake Forest），拍了些阔人的照片，还有他们住的那些大宅子。这个礼拜天想做小意大利。我们刚听说大伙儿在给您摆宴，里科先生，所以就寻思着……」
「行，」里科说，「麻利点儿。」

===Original===
“I’m out of this picture,” said the Big Boy, rising and walking over to the doorway. Sam Vettori took his place.
After manoeuvring about for a few minutes the photographer got the correct slant. He put the powder on the little tray.
“Now!” he cried.
Rico sat with his thumbs in the armholes of his vest, looking very stern. There was a blinding flash. Ottavio Vettori leapt into the air, and crying “My God, I’m shot” fell face down across the table. Everybody laughed.
When the newspaper men had gone, the Big Boy came over and put his hand on Rico’s arm.
“They may pick you up on that.”
===Chinese===
「这张照片我不掺和。」大老板说着起身，踱到门边。萨姆·维托里补了他的位子。
摄影记者比划了几分钟，找准了角度。他把镁粉倒在小托盘上。
「放！」他喊道。
里科两手拇指别在马甲腋口，端坐着，神情十分严肃。一道白光晃得人睁不开眼。奥塔维奥·维托里蹦了起来，喊了声「我的天，我中弹了」，脸朝下栽在桌子上。众人哄堂大笑。
报馆的人走后，大老板走过来，把手搭在里科胳膊上。
「没准儿他们就凭这张照片把你抓进去。」

===Original===
“Who the hell’s gonna see it.”
“You don’t know who’s gonna see it. That was a bad play, Rico.”
Rico laughed.
“If they pick me up, I’ll alibi them to death.”
When the banquet was over, Rico had Otero call him a cab. Blondy Belle was a little drunk and Rico had to support her as they went down the stairs. As she weighed about twenty pounds more than he did, this was not an easy job. As they were going out the side-entrance, Flaherty left his table in the club and came over to them.
He put his hand on Rico’s shoulder.
===Chinese===
「谁他妈会看那个。」
「你哪知道会落到谁眼里。这步棋走糟了，里科。」
里科笑了笑。
「他们要是抓我，我就拿不在场证明把他们活活耗死。」
宴会散场，里科让奥特罗去叫出租车。布朗迪·贝尔有点醉了，下楼时里科只得架着她。她比他足足重了二十来磅，这可不是件轻省差事。两人从侧门往外走时，弗莱厄蒂从夜总会里他的桌边起身，朝他们踱了过来。
他把手搭在里科肩上。

===Original===
“Getting up in the world ain’t you, Rico?”
Rico looked at him.
“Don’t you know your old pal Jim Flaherty?”
“Sure I know you. What’s the big idea?”
“Go chase yourself around the block, flatfoot,” said Blondy Belle; “if I ain’t getting sick of seeing bulls.”
“Hello, Blondy,” said Flaherty, “you and Rico hitting it off, eh? That’s the old ticket. Rico’s a good boy, but he’s young. If they don’t put him behind bars, he’ll be a man yet.”
===Chinese===
「混出息了，是不是，里科？」
里科看着他。
「不认得老朋友吉姆·弗莱厄蒂啦？」
「认得。你葫芦里卖的什么药？」
「滚一边遛弯去，臭条子，」布朗迪·贝尔说，「老娘见你们这群黑皮都快吐了。」
「你好啊，布朗迪，」弗莱厄蒂说，「跟里科处得热乎，嗯？这就对了。里科是好后生，就是年轻。只要别让人关进铁窗，往后是个人物。」

===Original===
“What’s the idea, Flaherty?” asked Rico.
“Why, I don’t want you to forget that I’m your friend,” said Flaherty. “I got my eyes on you, Rico. I like to see a young guy getting up in the world.”
“Yeah?” said Rico.
The cab was waiting at the curb and one of the waiters went out and opened the door for them. Rico boosted Blondy Belle into the cab. Flaherty stood in the doorway and watched them drive off.
===Chinese===
「你到底想干什么，弗莱厄蒂？」里科问。
「瞧，我不过是提醒你，别忘了我这个朋友，」弗莱厄蒂说，「我眼睛可一直盯着你，里科。我就爱看年轻人往上爬。」
「是吗？」里科说。
出租车在路边候着，一个侍者出去替他们拉开车门。里科把布朗迪·贝尔塞进车里。弗莱厄蒂站在门口，望着车子开走。

===Original===
“The nerve of that Irish bastard,” said Blondy.
But Rico had forgotten Flaherty. He sat thinking about Joe Massara. Gentleman Joe was getting too good for them, eh? He was going to turn softie.
“Well, I guess not,” said Rico.
===Chinese===
「那个爱尔兰杂种，好大的狗胆。」布朗迪说。
可里科早把弗莱厄蒂忘到脑后了。他坐在车上想着乔·马萨拉。绅士乔觉得自己高人一等了，嗯？他要当软蛋了。
「哼，我看未必。」里科说。

### IV / 四

===Original===
The sound of the pianola woke Rico. He sat up and looked at his wrist watch. It was two o’clock in the afternoon. He had slept twelve hours.
Rico lived at a tension. His nervous system was geared up to such a pitch that he was never sleepy, never felt the desire to relax, was always keenly alive. He did not average over five hours sleep a night and as soon as he opened his eyes he was awake. When he sat in a chair he never thrust out his feet and lolled, but sat rigid and alert. He walked, ate, took his pleasures in the same manner. What distinguished him from his associates was his inability to live in the present. He was like a man on a long train journey to a promised land. To him the present was but a dingy way-station; he had his eyes on the end of the journey.
Rico leapt out of bed and hastily put on his clothes.
“Twelve hours, boy,” he said to his reflection in the mirror, as he stood combing his hair, “that’ll never do.”
He had been seeing too much of Blondy Belle; that was the trouble. Rico had very little to do with women. He regarded them with a sort of contempt; they seemed so silly, reckless and purposeless, also mendacious and extremely undependable. Not that Rico trusted men, far from it. He was temperamentally suspicious. But in the course of his life he had discovered a few men he could trust, but no women. What he feared most in women, though, was not their treachery, that could be guarded against, but their ability to relax a man, to make him soft and slack, like Joe Massara. Rico had never been deeply involved with a woman. Incapable of tender sentiments, he had escaped the commoner kind of pitfalls. He was given to short bursts of lust, and this lust once satisfied, he looked at women impersonally for a while, as one looks at inanimate objects. But at times this lust, usually the result of an inner need and not the outcome of exterior stimulus, would be aroused by the sight of some particular woman. This had been the case with Blondy Belle; she was big, healthy and lascivious. This exactly suited Rico’s tastes; she excited him, and for that very reason he was on guard against her.
“Yeah,” he said, “I got to lay off Blondy for a while.”
===Chinese===
自动钢琴的声音把里科吵醒了。他坐起来看了看手表。下午两点。他睡了十二个钟头。
里科活得像根绷紧的弦。他的神经系统上得太紧，从不犯困，从不觉得想松口气，永远清醒得很。他夜里平均睡不到五个钟头，眼睛一睁，人就是全醒的。他坐椅子从不伸腿瘫靠，总是端坐着，绷着劲儿。走路、吃饭、找乐子，全是这副做派。他跟同伙不一样的地方，是他没法活在眼下。他像是个坐长途车奔应许之地的人。眼下对他不过是沿途一个灰扑扑的小站；他眼睛盯的是旅程的终点。
里科跳下床，急急忙忙穿上衣服。
「十二个钟头，小子，」他站在镜子前梳着头，对镜里的自己说，「这可不行。」
他跟布朗迪·贝尔厮混得太多；毛病就出在这上头。里科跟女人来往很少。他拿一种轻蔑看她们；她们显得那么蠢、那么没心没肺、那么没个正形，还满嘴谎话，靠也靠不住。倒不是说里科信得过男人，差得远。他天生多疑。可一辈子下来，他也数得出几个信得过的男人，女人却一个没有。不过他在女人身上最怕的不是背叛——那个防得住——而是她们能把一个男人泡软泡懈，叫他软塌下去，像乔·马萨拉那样。里科从来没有对哪个女人动过深感情。他不会温存，反倒躲开了那些常见的坑。他一阵阵地起性欲，这股欲一旦过去，有一阵子他看女人就跟看物件似的，不带感情。可有时候，这股欲——通常起于内里的需要，不是外头的撩拨——会让某个特定女人一眼勾起来。布朗迪·贝尔就是这种；她高大、健壮、风骚。这正对里科的胃口；她让他兴奋，也正因为这个，他防着她。
「是啊，」他说，「我得躲布朗迪一阵子。」

===Original===
She wanted him to come and live with her, but he refused. The offer tickled his vanity, though, for Pepi or Joe Sansone would have jumped at the chance. But not Rico.
He went out into the living room. Blondy, in a cerise kimono, was pedalling the pianola and singing loudly. The room was in disorder. Stockings hung from the backs of chairs, the dress Blondy had worn the night before was suspended from the chandelier on a coat-hanger, and there was a pile of clothes in the middle of the room.
Blondy turned around and smiled at him, pedalling the piano at the same time.
“What the hell kind of a piece is that?” asked Rico.
“That’s an Eyetalian piece,” said Blondy. “Ain’t it swell?”
“No,” said Rico, “I like jazz better.”
===Chinese===
她要他搬来同住，他回绝了。不过这份抬举倒挠着了他的虚荣心，换了佩皮或者乔·桑松，还不乐得扑上去。里科不。
他走进客厅。布朗迪穿着件樱桃红的和服晨袍，踩着自动钢琴的踏板，扯着嗓子唱歌。屋里一片狼藉。丝袜搭在椅背上，布朗迪前一晚穿的那条裙子用衣架吊在枝形吊灯上，屋子正中堆着一堆衣裳。
布朗迪回过头冲他笑，脚底下还踩着踏板。
「这他妈是什么曲子？」里科问。
「意大利曲子，」布朗迪说，「好听吧？」
「不好，」里科说，「我更爱爵士。」

===Original===
Blondy stopped the pianola and back-pedalled the roll.
“I got it yesterday because I thought you’d like it,” she said.
“Hell, quit kidding,” said Rico.
“I sure did. It’s from an opera.”
“Yeah? Say, what’s wrong with you?”
Blondy looked at him. She had pretensions. Ten years ago she had been a lady’s maid and she felt that she was somewhat cultured.
===Chinese===
布朗迪停住自动钢琴，把琴卷往回倒。
「我昨天特意弄来的，想着你会喜欢。」她说。
「少来这套。」里科说。
「真的。这是歌剧里的。」
「是吗？哎，你这人怎么回事？」
布朗迪看着他。她自命不凡。十年前她当过阔太太的贴身女仆，便自觉颇有几分教养。

===Original===
“You’d think I was a regular wop to hear you talk,” said Rico; “say, I was born in Youngstown and I can’t even speak the lingo.”
“Well, I guess I wasn’t born in the old country either,” said Blondy.
She put a new roll on the pianola and Rico sat smoking, while she played it. Rico had no ear for music; he couldn’t even whistle, or distinguish one tune from another. But he liked rhythm. There was something straightforward and primitive about jazz rhythms that impressed him.
“That’s a good one,” he said, when the roll was played through.
“Want to hear some more?”
“No,” said Rico, “I got to go.”
===Chinese===
「听你这话，我倒成地道意大利佬了，」里科说，「告诉你，我生在扬斯敦，连意大利话都不会讲。」
「得了，我也不是生在老家的。」布朗迪说。
她给自动钢琴换上一卷新琴卷，里科坐着抽烟，听她踩琴。里科对音乐没有耳朵；他连口哨都不会打，也分不清这支曲子那支曲子。可他喜欢节奏。爵士乐的节奏里有种直来直去的原始劲儿，正对他脾气。
「这支不错。」琴卷放完时他说。
「再来几支？」
「不听了，」里科说，「我得走了。」

===Original===
He rose and went over to the closet for his overcoat, but Blondy said:
“Listen, Rico, I want to see you a minute before you go.”
“What about?”
“About Little Arnie.”
Rico stared at her.
“What’s the idea? To hell with Little Arnie. As long as he’s straight with me I ain’t got no interest in him at all.”
===Chinese===
他起身去衣橱拿大衣，布朗迪却说：
「听着，里科，你走之前我有话跟你说。」
「什么事？」
「小阿尼的事。」
里科盯着她。
「搞什么名堂？小阿尼去他的。只要他对我没有二心，我对他半点兴趣也没有。」

===Original===
“He ain’t straight with nobody.”
Rico just looked at her.
Little Arnie had played his hand badly. At first he hadn’t minded losing Blondy Belle in the least; she cost him a good deal of money and she bored and irritated him. But he had been kidded unmercifully. As he had no sense of humour whatever and was very touchy in a personal matter, this eventually angered him. In revenge, he talked. He told all who would listen that Blondy Belle was a liar, a crook, and had certain unnatural appetites. Killer Pepi was one of the auditors and he immediately repeated Little Arnie’s assertions to his woman, Blue Jay, who ran at once to Blondy Belle. Yes, Little Arnie, who was fifty percent fool, had played his hand badly.
Blondy lit a cigarette and lay down on the davenport.
“Come over here and sit down,” she said; “I’ll give you an earful.”
“I ain’t got no time,” said Rico.
===Chinese===
「他对谁都不老实。」
里科只是看着她。
小阿尼这手牌打砸了。起初丢了布朗迪·贝尔他一点也不心疼；这女人花他不少钱，还招他烦惹他躁。可旁人拿他开涮开得没了边。他本来一点幽默感也没有，私事上又格外碰不得，一来二去就火了。报复的法子，就是一张嘴到处说。他逢人就讲：布朗迪·贝尔是个骗子、贼货，还有几样见不得人的怪癖。杀手佩皮是听客之一，转头就把小阿尼这套话学给了他的女人蓝松鸦，蓝松鸦立马跑去找布朗迪·贝尔。可不是嘛，小阿尼这个半吊子傻子，这手牌打砸了。
布朗迪点了支烟，在长榻上躺下。
「过来这边坐下，」她说，「我有的是话讲给你听。」
「我没工夫。」里科说。

===Original===
Blondy blew out a cloud of smoke.
“What you got on your mind?” said Rico; “spill it.”
“All right,” said Blondy. “Arnie’s giving you a split on the house, ain’t he? What’s the split?”
“Thirty percent.”
“How do you know you’re getting thirty?”
“I look at the books.”
===Chinese===
布朗迪喷出一口烟。
「你葫芦里卖的什么药？」里科说，「倒出来。」
「行，」布朗迪说，「阿尼分你赌场的份子，是吧？几分？」
「三成。」
「你怎么知道真是三成？」
「我查账本。」

===Original===
Blondy laughed.
“Them books is crooked.”
“Straight dope?” asked Rico, his face hardening.
“Sure,” said Blondy, “I wasn’t gonna say nothing. It wasn’t none of my business, but Arnie’s been peddling a lot of loose talk about me and I don’t take that.”
“All right,” said Rico, “now you know so damn much, how we gonna prove it?”
“It’s a cinch,” said Blondy; “hand Arnie’s boy, Joe Peeper, some dough and he’ll spill the news. Joe hates Arnie.”
===Chinese===
布朗迪笑了。
「那账本是假的。」
「消息属实？」里科问，脸色沉了下来。
「属实，」布朗迪说，「我本不想多嘴。这不关我的事，可阿尼到处嚼我的舌根，这口气我咽不下。」
「行，」里科说，「既然你他妈知道这么多，咱们怎么拿出凭据？」
「容易得很，」布朗迪说，「给阿尼的手下乔·皮珀塞几个钱，他就全招了。乔恨阿尼。」

===Original===
“Good!” said Rico, banging the table with his fist; “I’ll run Arnie out of town and declare you in, Blondy. You got brains.”
Blondy looked at him.
“You stick to me, boy, and we’ll own the town.”
“Don’t get swelled up,” said Rico, “just because you happened to be in the know.”
That’s what she liked about Rico. He was hard to impress.
“Hell of a lot of thanks I get for it,” said Blondy.
===Chinese===
「好！」里科说，一拳砸在桌上，「我把阿尼撵出城，让你入伙，布朗迪。你有脑子。」
布朗迪看着他。
「你跟着我，小子，这座城迟早归咱们。」
「别烧包，」里科说，「不过碰巧知道点内幕而已。」
这正是布朗迪喜欢里科的地方。他不容易被撩得飘起来。
「好心当了驴肝肺。」布朗迪说。

===Original===
“Don’t worry about that,” said Rico, his head buzzing with projects, “you’ll get something better than thanks.”
Rico went to the closet and got his coat and hat.
“Wait a minute, big boy,” said Blondy, “you ain’t heard it all. Listen, that joint of Arnie’s is worth plenty of dough. He ain’t gonna give it up without a battle.”
“Hell,” said Rico, “he’s yellow.”
“Sure he is. But he’s tricky. Rico, if you can’t work the Joe Peeper stunt, here’s a lever. Remember Limpy John?”
“Sure,” said Rico, “they bumped him off.”
===Chinese===
「这你放心，」里科说，脑子里盘算得嗡嗡响，「到手的东西会比一句谢实在。」
里科走到衣橱前，取了大衣和帽子。
「等会儿，大个子，」布朗迪说，「你还没听全呢。听着，阿尼那家场子值老鼻子钱了。他不打一仗是不会撒手的。」
「嗐，」里科说，「他是个软蛋。」
「他是。可他阴。里科，乔·皮珀那条道要是走不通，这儿还有根撬棍。记得瘸子约翰（Limpy John）吗？」
「记得，」里科说，「他们把他干掉了。」

===Original===
“Who did?”
“The cops.”
Blondy laughed.
“They thought they did. Arnie bumped him off.”
Rico grinned.
“I got you.”
===Chinese===
「谁干的？」
「条子。」
布朗迪笑了。
「他们自以为是他们干的。是阿尼干掉的。」
里科咧嘴一笑。
「我明白了。」

===Original===
Rico put on his overcoat.
“Be around tonight?” asked Blondy.
“No. I got business.”
“Monkey business.”
“No, I got to go cross town. I’ll give you a ring tomorrow.”
Blondy lay back on the davenport.
===Chinese===
里科穿上大衣。
「今晚来吗？」布朗迪问。
「不来。有事。」
「野鸳鸯的事吧。」
「不是，我得去城那头。明天给你打电话。」
布朗迪仰面躺回长榻。

===Original===
“You’ll sure be missing something,” she said.
“I’ll ketch up,” said Rico.
When Rico had gone, Blondy played a couple of rolls on the pianola, then she drank half a pint of liquor and went back to bed.
===Chinese===
「那你可要错过好东西了。」她说。
「回头补上。」里科说。
里科走后，布朗迪又踩了两卷琴，然后灌下半品脱酒，回床上去了。

### V / 五

===Original===
Rico found the door of his apartment unlocked. Before entering he unbuttoned his overcoat and took out his automatic. Only one person had a key to his apartment except himself: Otero. If Otero wasn’t in there then whoever was in there was in trouble. Rico opened the door slowly. Otero was sitting with his chair tipped back against the wall, smoking a cigarette and dozing.
“Otero!”
Otero opened his eyes.
“Hello, boss.”
Rico locked the door behind him.
“Listen, don’t you know better than to leave that door open?”
===Chinese===
里科发现公寓的门没锁。进门前他解开大衣，掏出自动手枪。除了他自己，只有一个人有他公寓的钥匙：奥特罗。要是里头不是奥特罗，那里面的人就要倒霉了。里科缓缓推开门。奥特罗正仰在椅子里，椅背抵着墙，抽着烟打盹。
「奥特罗！」
奥特罗睁开眼。
「你好，老大。」
里科回身锁上门。
「听着，门就这么敞着，你就不知道这有多悬？」

===Original===
“I forgot, Rico.”
Rico took off his overcoat and hat.
“You better keep your head working, boy,” said Rico, “or you’ll get your neck stretched. What you doing here, anyway?”
Otero got up from his chair and stood dangling his hat.
“I want money.”
Rico looked at him.
===Chinese===
「我忘了，里科。」
里科脱下大衣和帽子。
「你脑子最好转起来，小子，」里科说，「不然迟早叫人把你的脖子绞长喽。你到底来干什么？」
奥特罗从椅子上起身站着，帽子在手里晃荡。
「我要钱。」
里科看着他。

===Original===
“I’m broke boss. I ain’t got a cent.”
Rico laughed. Otero seemed so helpless.
“You mean to tell me you ain’t got a cent out of that Casa Alvarado split?”
Otero shrugged.
“Well, Seal she spends money, spends money. I take it out of my pocket till I ain’t got any more.” Otero shrugged and rolled a fresh cigarette.
Rico took out his billfold and handed Otero a fifty.
===Chinese===
「我破产了，老大。一个子儿都没了。」
里科笑了。奥特罗显得那么没辙。
「你是说，卡萨·阿尔瓦拉多那票分下来的钱，你一个子儿都不剩了？」
奥特罗耸耸肩。
「唉，海豹她花钱，一个劲儿地花。我就从兜里往外掏，掏到兜里空了。」奥特罗耸耸肩，又卷了一支烟。
里科掏出钱夹，递给奥特罗一张五十的。

===Original===
“I’ll take that out of your next split.”
Otero smiled.
“That’s all the same to me, boss.”
He was speaking the truth. He hadn’t the slightest conception of the value of money. He spent till what he had was gone, then he asked Rico for more. Rico shook his head.
“Listen, Otero, ain’t you never gonna get no sense! You got over a grand and a half out of that Casa Alvarado stand. And here you are broke. Why some guys work a whole year for less than that.”
Otero shrugged.
===Chinese===
「这钱从你下回份子里扣。」
奥特罗笑了。
「对我来说都一样，老大。」
他讲的是实话。钱值多少，他一点概念也没有。有钱就花，花光了，就再找里科要。里科摇了摇头。
「听着，奥特罗，你就永远长不出脑子吗！卡萨·阿尔瓦拉多那一场你分了一千五百多块。现在落了个精光。多少人干一整年还挣不来这个数。」
奥特罗耸耸肩。

===Original===
“I have worked for two pesos a week.”
Rico took some small change out of his pocket and handed it to Otero.
“Go down to the corner and get a couple of *Tribune*s. Get three.”
“Three of the same kind?”
“Sure.”
Otero went out. Rico opened the window a few inches and sat down beside it. There was a touch of spring in the air and it made him feel restless. He wanted to be doing things. In a week or less, he’d have Little Arnie’s big gambling joint. That meant dough and plenty of it. He’d turn it over to Sam Vettori and let him run it. Sam was looking for something to do. Then maybe he could muscle in on the North Side graft. That wasn’t easy. Pete Montana was a wise bird and he had the North Side tied up. Well, maybe the Big Boy could help him there. Rico jumped to his feet and began to pace up and down.
===Chinese===
「我从前一礼拜才挣两个比索。」
里科从兜里掏出些零钱，递给奥特罗。
「去街角买两份《论坛报》（Tribune）。买三份。」
「三份一样的？」
「对。」
奥特罗出去了。里科把窗子推开几寸，坐在窗边。空气里有了几分春意，搅得他心浮气躁。他想干事情。不出一个礼拜，小阿尼那家大赌场就归他了。那意味着钱，大把的钱。他会把它交给萨姆·维托里打理。萨姆正闲得没事干。再往后，没准还能往北区的油水里插一脚。那不容易。皮特·蒙大那是只老鸟，北区让他攥得死死的。嗯，这方面兴许大老板能帮他。里科一跃而起，来回踱起步来。

===Original===
Otero came in with the papers. Rico took them from him and tore one of them apart till he came to the magazine section. There it was. Big type proclaimed:

> Italian underworld chief given big feed

Otero, looking over Rico’s shoulder, saw the flashlight picture. In his excitement he pushed Rico aside and placing his finger on a section of the picture, cried:
“There I am!”
===Chinese===
奥特罗拿着报纸回来了。里科接过来，把其中一份撕开，翻到副刊版。就在那儿。大号标题赫然写着：

> 意大利黑帮头目豪宴宾朋

奥特罗从里科肩后探头，看见了那张镁光灯照片。他激动起来，一把拨开里科，手指戳在照片的一角，喊道：
「我在这里！」

===Original===
Rico took the other two papers apart and got out the magazine sections. Then he put the three sections side by side and compared them.
“All too dark,” he said.
Nevertheless, having chosen the clearest one of the three, he took his scissors and cut it out.
“I want one too,” said Otero.
“All right,” said Rico, “help yourself.”
===Chinese===
里科把另外两份报纸也拆开，抽出副刊。他把三份副刊并排摆开，逐一比对。
「都太暗。」他说。
话虽这么说，他还是挑了三份里最清楚的那份，拿起剪刀剪了下来。
「我也想要一张。」奥特罗说。
「行，」里科说，「自己动手。」

### VI / 六

===Original===
DeVoss was standing in the lobby when Rico came in. DeVoss looked him over thoroughly, positive that he was out of his element in an atmosphere as exclusive as that of The Bronze Peacock. Not that Rico looked the least bit shabby. If anything, he was dressed more carefully than usual, from his modish derby to his fawn-coloured spats. The big ulster he was wearing hid the loud striped suit and a plain dark muffler hid the loud striped tie. No, sartorially Rico could pass at The Bronze Peacock. But there was something vulgar and predatory about him that did not escape DeVoss.
“That’s a bad one there,” he told himself.
Rico glanced about the lobby, taking everything in from habit. It was not a good plant but it could be worked. Not that he had any intention of working it, but you never know. He came up to DeVoss and said:
“Excuse me, but where’ll I find the manager of this place.”
DeVoss looked at him coldly.
“I’m the manager.”
===Chinese===
里科进门时，德沃斯正站在门厅里。德沃斯把他从头到脚细细打量，认定他跟「青铜孔雀」（The Bronze Peacock）这种高门坎场子的气场格格不入。倒不是说里科有半点寒酸。若说有什么，他这身穿得比平时更讲究，从时新的圆顶礼帽到浅黄鞋罩，样样齐整。身上那件长大衣罩住了扎眼的条纹西服，一条素色深围巾遮住了花哨的条纹领带。不，就衣着论，里科在青铜孔雀过得去关。可他身上那股粗野的、食肉动物似的东西，德沃斯看得清清楚楚。
「这可不是善茬儿。」他心里说。
里科扫了一眼门厅，习惯地把一切都收进眼里。这买卖底子不算好，但能做。倒不是他真打算做它，可谁知道呢。他走到德沃斯跟前，说：
「劳驾，请问这儿的经理在哪儿能找到？」
德沃斯冷冷地看他。
「我就是经理。」

===Original===
Rico grinned.
“Well,” he said, “I guess we got a mutual friend. The Big Boy tells me you and him does business together.”
DeVoss’s manner changed abruptly.
“Oh, yes. You’re one of his friends, are you? What can I do for you?”
“I want to see Joe Massara.”
“That’s easy,” said DeVoss, “he’s back in his dressing-room. I’ll take you back.”
===Chinese===
里科咧嘴一笑。
「那好，」他说，「咱们大概有位共同的朋友。大老板跟我说，你跟他有生意往来。」
德沃斯的态度陡然变了。
「哦，是吗。您是他的朋友？我能为您效什么劳？」
「我要见乔·马萨拉。」
「这容易，」德沃斯说，「他在后面化妆间。我带您去。」

===Original===
Rico followed DeVoss, and they went up a few steps at the end of the lobby and came out into the club proper. It was empty except for a couple of electricians who were working on the stage spotlights.
“So you’re one of the Big Boy’s friends,” said DeVoss, curious.
“I’m Rico.”
DeVoss looked at him, startled.
“Oh,” he said, “you’re Rico.”
All the way up the rear corridor DeVoss kept looking sideways at Rico. One of Little Arnie’s men had told him about the new Vettori gang chief. Dangerous as dynamite!
===Chinese===
里科跟着德沃斯，在门厅尽头上了几级台阶，进了夜总会正厅。场子里空荡荡的，只有几个电工在调舞台的聚光灯。
「这么说您是大老板的朋友。」德沃斯说，心里好奇。
「我是里科。」
德沃斯看着他，吃了一惊。
「哦，」他说，「您就是里科。」
一路穿过后走廊，德沃斯不住拿眼角瞟里科。小阿尼手下的一个人跟他说起过维托里帮的新头目。跟炸药一样危险！

===Original===
DeVoss knocked at Joe’s door. Someone called “come in.” DeVoss opened the door and Rico followed him into the room. Joe was sitting in his shirt sleeves, his vest off, displaying a pair of fancy suspenders. (Rico made a mental note of the suspenders. His taste ran more to fancy sleeve garters.
